---

> Ntụaka zuru ezu maka environment variable ọ bụla OmniRoute na-amata.
> Maka template mbido-ngwa-ngwa, lee [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Variable ọ bụla edepụtara ebe a ga-apụtakwa na `.env.example`, variable ọ bụla dị na
> `.env.example` ga-apụtakwa ebe a. `npm run check:env-doc-sync`
> na-amanye nke a mgbe a na-eme commit nakwa na CI. Iji kpachapụ variable ụma, tinye ya na
> allowlist dị n'ime `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Nzuzo Achọrọ

Nzuzo ndị etinyere akara **Ee** nke a na-apụghị ịtọgharị ezubere iche na mgbakwunye mbu (`JWT_SECRET`, `API_KEY_SECRET`) ma debe na `server.env` n'ime nchekwa data — debe ha kpọmkwem mgbe nchekwa ahụ bụ nke na-enweghị nkwụnye. Ihe nzuzo pụrụ iche bụ `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET`: a naghị echekwa ya na `server.env` — ebe ntinye sava na-emepụta uru nke a na-ejikarị naanị site n'ịkekọrịta nke na-arụ ọrụ n'ime usoro, ya mere ndị ahịa eriri mpụga ga-achọ ka edobe ya kpọmkwem.

| Variable                     | Achọrọ                 | Ndabara                            | Faịlụ Isi                                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------------- | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Ee**                 | _(none)_                           | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Na-edekọ / na-enyocha kuki nzukọ dashboard (JWT). Mepụta ya na `openssl rand -base64 48`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Ee**                 | _(a na-emepụta ya na-akpaghị aka)_ | `src/shared/utils/apiKey.ts`                              | Ihe nzuzo a na-eji emepụta ma nyochaa checksum nke igodo API ejikọrọ na igwe. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, usoro mmalite nkịtị na-emepụta ya ma chekwaa ya na `server.env` ma ọ bụrụ na enwere ike ide n'ime faịlụ ahụ. Debe ya kpọmkwem ma ọ bụrụ na ndekọ data bụ nke nwa oge. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | Mba                    | _(unset)_                          | `src/lib/auth/managementPassword.ts`                      | Na-etinye okwuntughe dashboard na mmalite ebe okwuntughe adịghị echekwara; a na-efunahụ ya ozugbo echekwara okwuntughe. Ntụrụ npm na nsụgharị isi mmalite na-akopi `.env.example` gaa na `.env`, ya mere ha na-amalite na akara ngosi a mara nke ọma `CHANGEME`: dochie ya tupu boot mbụ, ma ọ bụ debe `INITIAL_PASSWORD=` (efu) na `.env` ahụ iji mepụta okwuntughe na onye nduzi nbata n'oge nleta mbụ, dịka agwa echekwara (ndabara nke oyiyi Docker). Gbanwee okwuntughe echekwara na Dashboard → Settings → Security, ma ọ bụ gbaa `omniroute reset-password` (`node bin/reset-password.mjs` na nchịkọta isi mmalite) na nke a etinyere `DATA_DIR` n'ọnọdụ nchekwa data nke sava. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Ee** (n'ọrụ mmepụta) | _(unset)_                          | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Nzuzo nke ejikọtara maka eriri WebSocket Codex Responses nke ime ụlọ. Na-enyocha arịrịọ eriri n'etiti Electron/browser WS relay na OmniRoute. ⚠️ **ACHỌRỌ n'ọrụ mmepụta — mgbe etinyebeghị, a na-ajụ arịrịọ WS bridge niile.** Mepụta ya na `openssl rand -base64 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | Mba                    | _(git SHA)_                        | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Njirimara doro anya maka iwepu cache nke service-worker maka PWA shell (#11779); nke mbụ n'usoro ngwọta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | Mba                    | _(unset)_                          | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Nke abụọ na usoro — etinyere site n'aka ndị na-ewu PaaS (dịka Heroku-style) dịka nkwenye etinyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | Mba                    | _(derived)_                        | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Uru ọha nke wụrụ n'ọrụ mgbe wụrụ nke onye ahịa ji debanye aha `/sw.js?v=…`; sitere na nke abụọ n'elu, mgbe ahụ na-esote git SHA.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | Mba (akpaghị aka)      | _(auto per boot)_                  | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Nzuzo nke usoro ọ bụla na-egosi na akara onye mmekọ IP a tụkwasịrị obi sitere na sava HTTP nke OmniRoute (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Middleware authz na-ekwere ebe arịrịọ si (loopback / LAN gating nke ụzọ LOCAL_ONLY) naanị mgbe akara ahụ nwere token a. A na-emepụta ya akpaghị aka kwa boot — hapụ ya unset; debe ya naanị maka nhazi usoro ọtụtụ usoro nke ga-ekekọrịta akara ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`  | Mba                    | _(unset)_                          | `scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`                              | IPs ma ọ bụ ogo CIDR nke reverse proxies kewapụrụ site na kọma nke `X-Forwarded-For` / `X-Real-IP` nke ndepụta ikikere/mkpagbu IP nwere ike kweta, tinyere loopback, adreesị netwọkụ nzuzo na Cloudflare edges, nke a na-ekwere mgbe niile. A chọrọ ya naanị maka proxy nọ na adreesị ọha ọ bụla ọzọ; ma ọ bụghị ya, adreesị nke proxy ahụ onwe ya bụ nke ihe nzacha na-atụle. Throttle nke onye ahịa nke relay (`/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`) na-eji otu adreesị onye ahịa a dozie, ọ bụghị naanị na isi ozi nnyefe.                                                                                                                                                              |

### Iwu Mepụta

```bash
# Mepụta nzuzo anọ niile ozugbo:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!NTỊAKA]
> Egbula commit faịlụ `.env` nke nwere ezi nzuzo n'ịchịkwa njikwa ụdị. Faịlụ `.gitignore` arụla ọrụ nke ịkpọpụ `.env`, mana jide n'aka tupu ịtọgharị.

## 2. Nchekwa & Ebe Nchekwa Data

OmniRoute na-eji **SQLite** (site na `better-sqlite3`) maka nchekwa data niile na-adịgide adịgide. Mgbanwe ndị a na-achịkwa ebe data dị, izo ya ezo, na usoro ndụ ya.

| Mgbanwe                                    | Ndabara                       | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Ndekọ mgbọrọgwụ maka DB SQLite, nkwado ndabere, na faịlụ data. Gbanwee ya maka volume Docker ma ọ bụ ụzọ ahaziri iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Ụzọ mgbapụ maka ihe nche DATA_DIR nke ule/ntụle (#10428). A na-ebuga ule na nyocha eval/print nke Node (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, gụnyere ụdị `--eval=`/`--print=`) ndị na-enweghị `DATA_DIR` gaa na ndekọ nwa oge a ga-atụfu atụfu ka ha ghara imepe ebe nchekwa data n'ezie nke onye na-ahụ maka sistemụ; debe ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji họrọ iji ezigbo ndekọ ahụ ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA nke artifact na-arụ ọrụ. `npm run build:release` na-akanye ya; enwere ike ịtinye ya n'ime container ndị ebufere na-enweghị sentinel `dist/BUILD_SHA`. A na-egosipụta ya dịka `system.buildSha` na `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref nke ọnụ ụzọ nyocha mmalite artifact ngwugwu ji enyocha SHA nke build megide ya (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(edobeghị)_                  | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Debe ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji kwe ka a kwakọba build nke SHA ya na-anọghị n'ahịrị release, ma dekọọ ya dịka canary e bu n'obi kama ime ka ọnụ ụzọ nyocha ahụ daa (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(edobeghị)_                  | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | Igodo API maka nyocha smoke nke canary-deploy, nke a na-eziga dịka `Authorization: Bearer` na `/v1/chat/completions`. Ọ bụ naanị script mbugharị (#10429) na-eji ya, sava anaghị eji ya ma ọlị. Ọ naghị emetụta mgbanwe `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` nke ngwa nyocha smoke CLI a na-ahọrọ iji (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` dị na `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — lee [Njikọ CLI → Nnyocha smoke n'ezie](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Ihe mgbaàmà maka usoro build (#10060): `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`, worker build ọ bụla e mepụtara na-eketakwa ya, ka oyi akwa DB wee weghachite stub na-adịghị eme ihe kama ibunye addon better-sqlite3 nke native (nke na-akwụsị worker mgbe ọ na-apụ). A naghị edobe ya ma ọlị maka sava na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Debe ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji wụfee nyocha prebuild nke dependency native a na-ahọrọ ime maka osisi vendored ndị pụrụ iche. Nke a anaghị eme ka dependency na-efu nwee ike ịrụ build; jiri ya naanị mgbe a na-eweta dependency native site n'ụzọ ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(edobeghị)_                  | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Aha ọzọ ndabere** maka `DATA_DIR`, nke a na-enyocha naanị mgbe edobeghị `DATA_DIR`. A na-eji ya achọta cache session thread-sticky nke executor PromptQL dị na diski (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); ọ bụrụ na edobeghị mgbanwe abụọ ahụ, cache ahụ ga-anọ naanị na memory (agaghị echekwa ya n'etiti mmalitegharịa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(edobeghị)_                  | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Directory nke **runtime plugin scanner** na-agụ — nakwa root ebe plugin manager na-etinye ihe — nke na-akagbu ndabara sitere na home (#11827). Tụọ ya aka n'osisi plugin e ji bind mount na Docker/K8s kama ịkwaga HOME naanị iji gbanwee ebe scan path dị (HOME na-achịkwa omume ndị ọzọ niile metụtara home). Mgbe edobeghị ya = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ma ọ bụ `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` mgbe process ahụ anaghị ebupụ home ọ bụla ma ọlị — nke a na-ewepụ ọnọdụ ebe a naghị achọpụta ihe n'enweghị ọkwa. A na-edekọ directory e kpebisiri ike otu ugboro mgbe mmalite dịka `scanner.dir_resolved`, yana input nke meriri. Ọ bụ naanị n'akụkụ sava: plugin iwu CLI ka na-eji `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` nke ha (ngalaba 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(efu efu = agbanyụrụ)_       | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | Igodo AES maka izochi database SQLite niile mgbe echekwara ya. Jiri `openssl rand -hex 32` mepụta ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Label ụdị maka igodo izochi ahụ. Welie ya mgbe ị na-atụgharị igodo ka e nwee ike ịtọpụ izochi backup ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, ọ na-awụfe nkwado ndabere faịlụ SQLite a na-eme mgbe niile/tupu ide ihe (ichekwa/ihichapụ ọnụahịa models.dev, ide ntọala). Nkwado ndabere ejiri aka mee na nke tupu iweghachi ka na-arụ ọrụ. Ọ **gaghị** agbanyụọ snapshot nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide nke onye na-eme migration ga-emerịrị ma ọ bụ ihe nchebe mass-migration maka DB na-adịgide adịgide dịbu adị. A na-amachi nkwado ndabere ndị a na-ejighị aka eme ka ha ghara ịgafe otu ugboro n'ime nkeji 60. **Ntọala → Nchekwa** nke Dashboard nwere ike ịgbanyụọ nkwado ndabere akpaka a na-eme mgbe niile n'onwe ya.                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_SLOW_MS`                 | _(edoghị/agbanyụrụ)_          | `src/lib/db/adapters/slowDiagnostics.ts`              | Milisekọnd; ụkpụrụ ndị na-erughị 1 ma ọ bụ ndị na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-anọgide na-agbanyụrụ; ọ na-adọ aka ná ntị naanị otu ugboro kachasị n'ime 10s; ahịrị ndekọ nwere naanị driver, operation, na elapsedMs.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_OPTIMIZE_ENABLED`        | `true`                        | `src/lib/db/sqlitePlannerStats.ts`                    | Nlekọta ọnụ ọgụgụ planner na ndabere, nke na-adabereghị na nhicha dabere n'ogologo oge nchekwa: ngafe nhicha mbụ a gbanyere (ọ na-abụkarị 30s mgbe mmalite gasịrị, mgbe migrations gasịrị) na-eme `PRAGMA optimize=0x10002`; ngafe ndị na-esote kwa 6h na-eme `PRAGMA optimize` (ma ọ bụ `PRAGMA optimize=0x00002` maka nyocha na-enweghị oke). `false` ma ọ bụ `0` na-agbanyụ nlekọta niile (a naghị ekewa mkpụrụedemede ukwu na nta, a na-ewepụkwa oghere dị n'akụkụ). Ọ naghị emetụta ebe nchekwa data dị na memory ma ọ bụ ụzọ ndabere e nwere ike ịgụ naanị. A na-edekọ ọdịda na log, a naghị atụ exceptions.                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_ANALYSIS_LIMIT`          | `400`                         | `src/lib/db/sqlitePlannerStats.ts`                    | Na-etinye `PRAGMA analysis_limit` tupu nkwalite. A na-ejedebe ọnụọgụ zuru ezu nke sistemụ iri na [0, 100000]; uru nwere nhazi na-ezighị ezi ma ọ bụ ndị tọgbọ chakoo na-eji uru ndabara 400. `0` na-arịọ nyocha ziri ezi na-enweghị oke nke tebụl nkwalite ahụ họọrọ; ngafe oge na-ewepụ bit nke oke sampling nke SQLite optimize iji soro nhọrọ a. A naghị eme iwu SQL nyocha nọọrọ onwe ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Aha ọzọ ochie** maka `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. A na-anabata ya dị ka nhọrọ ndabere mgbe variable bụ isi adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | A na-anabata **aha ọzọ ochie** (ụdị e jiri Base64 koodu) dị ka nhọrọ ndabere. A na-ewepụ koodu ya na-akpaghị aka tupu ojiji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Dochie oge etiti nyocha ahụike SQLite a na-eme mgbe niile (ms). Mgbe edoghị ya, a na-enweta ụkpụrụ ndabara site na `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(ewepụla)_                   | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **Ewepụla ya.** `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` a na-eme mgbe niile n'oge sistemụ na-arụ ọrụ nwere ike ime ka mapping wal-index a na-ekekọrịta ghara ịdị irè ma jiri SIGBUS (#13973) kwụsị process ahụ na mberede, ya mere scheduler ahụ adịghịzi. Variable ahụ anaghị arụ ọrụ: ụkpụrụ dị mma na-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị otu ugboro na agakwaghị eji ya, ebe `0` ma ọ bụ ọnọdụ edoghị ya anaghị ekwu ihe ọ bụla. A na-elekọta WAL site na checkpoint PASSIVE (n'okpuru), checkpoint mgbe a na-eme shutdown na-eme ka ọ dị mkpụmkpụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (5m)                 | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Dochie oge etiti `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` a na-eme ugboro ugboro (ms). Ọ na-eme ka frame WAL ndị na-echere dị ole na ole ka checkpoint wee nọgide na-adị ngwa, meekwa ka nha faịlụ WAL ghara ịgafe oke n'etiti mbelata ndị a na-eme n'oge shutdown. `0` na-agbanyụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                         | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Mgbe tick PASSIVE chọpụtara na faịlụ WAL karịrị nha a, mee `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` ka WAL malitegharịa n’edoghachighị wal-index e mapụtara. E wepụrụ checkpoints nke truncate-mode na-arụ ọrụ ozugbo (lee ahịrị `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Tọọ ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji kwụsị process ahụ mgbe nrụgide akụ dị oke njọ gara n’ihu ruo `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, ka supervisor (systemd `Restart=always`, iwu restart nke Docker) nwee ike iweghachite process dị ọcha kama ịga n’ihu na-enye 503 ruo mgbe ebighị ebi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (nkeji 2)            | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Ogologo oge nrụgide dị oke njọ ga-anọgide tupu nkwụsị maka restart nke onwe ya amalite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_PSI_DISABLED`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressurePolicy.ts`            | Tọọ ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji leghara `/proc/pressure/memory` (`psi_some` / `psi_full`) nke host dum anya mgbe onye agbata obi na-eme mkpọtụ kpalitere 503 ma process a ka dị mma (#13124). Ihe nchedo cgroup na heap V8 ka na-arụ ọrụ. PSI agaghịkwa agụta dị ka ihe akaebe kernel ji egosi throttle maka `cgroup_high`: ngụkọta cgroup karịrị `memory.high` ga-abụzi nke dị oke njọ naanị mgbe `memory.events` `high`/`max` bawanyere. Ọ bụrụ na `memory.events` adịghị, a ga-edobe raw ratio ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _(nchọpụta akpaghị aka)_      | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Path akọwapụtara kpọmkwem (absolute ma ọ bụ relative na cwd) gaa `sql-wasm.wasm` mgbe a na-eji adapter ndabere WASM nke `sql.js`. Mgbe a na-edoghị ya, a na-achọpụta ya akpaghị aka site na dependencies nke package na nhazi ndị nwere ike ịbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                          | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Ọnụọgụ ụbọchị a ga-edobe checkpoints, faịlụ input/output/error ndị e zoro aka na ha, na row nke ọrụ Batch API ruru njedebe (emechara/dara/akagbuola/agafeela oge) tupu automatic cleanup sweep ehichapụ ha. Ọ na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ naanị mgbe agbanyere `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`; ọ dabara na oge OpenAI ji edobe output nke Batch API nke ya. Ọ naghị emetụta route `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` nke operator kpalitere, nke na-anọgide na-enweghị ọnọdụ ọ bụla (enweghị age filter) dịka e siri hazie ya.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, kwe ka automatic cleanup sweep hichapụ ọrụ Batch API ruru njedebe (na checkpoints ha) ndị gafere `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, ma kpochapụ ọdịnaya BLOB nke faịlụ e bulitere mgbe ha gafere `expires_at` nke ha. A gbanyụrụ ya na ndabara: install niile dị ugbu a ga-edobe data a otu ọ dịbu ruo mgbe operator họọrọ ịgbanye ya. Ọ bụkwa feature flag enwere ike idezi na dashboard — lee `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Oge ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafere nyocha ahụike DB kpamkpam mgbe a na-amalite. Ọ bara uru maka ọrụ ndị na-adịru obere oge na ule njikọta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji manye loop nyocha ahụike DB ka ọ rụọ ọrụ, ọbụlagodi mgbe a gaara amafere ya n'ọnọdụ nkịtị (dịka ọmụmaatụ, ọrụ ndị na-adịru obere oge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                           | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji mafere nkwadebe native-runtime mgbe a na-eme `npm install`. Ọ bara uru na nrụnye CI/headless ebe arụlarị sqlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _(nchọpụta-akpaka)_           | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Dochie ndekọ ebe migration runner na-enyocha. Ọ bara uru mgbe a na-ezipụ migration ndị a chịkọtara ọnụ n'ime build ahaziri iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Ndekọ migration ndị ọzọ dị ka ntinye `namespace=dir` nke ihe nkewa path nke ikpo okwu kewapụrụ (dịka ọmụmaatụ `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). A na-edekọ faịlụ ndị a hụrụ ebe ahụ dị ka `<namespace>-<number>`, ka nkesa nke na-ebuga migration nke ya ghara ịdakọrịta na oghere ọnụọgụ nke upstream. Ntinye na-ezighị ezi, namespace na-adịghị irè, ma ọ bụ ndekọ na-adịghị adị ga-ebute njehie mgbe a na-amalite kama ịmafe schema ahụ nwayọ n'enweghị ọkwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Oke nchekwa maka migration dị ukwuu na-echere (#3416). Mmalite ga-akwụsị ma ọ bụrụ na migration ndị na-echere karịrị ọnụọgụ a na DB dị adị (nke a na-echebe megide tebụl nsuso ehichapụrụ). Bulie ya iji weghachite backup ochie; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ nyocha ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_ | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Ndekọ ọrụ maka ọnụ ụzọ mwepụta `check:install-upgrade`. Ọ chọrọ ihe dị ka 12 GB (osisi nrụnye abụọ nke ihe dị ka 3 GB n'otu n'otu tinyere tarball), ya mere, ọ gaghị arụ ọrụ na tmpfs pere mpe — na runner nke a na-elekọta n'onwe ya, `/tmp` bụ tmpfs 12 GB nke RAM na-akwado, ọnụ ụzọ ahụ mekwara ka ohere ya gwụ, nke mere ka package ahụ ghara izu oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(ndabara dị na koodu)_       | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Oge etiti flush (ms) maka onye na-ede mmefu/ọnụahịa n'ìgwè. Uru ndị dị ala na-ebelata ijikọ ndegharị ọnụ; uru ndị dị elu na-ebelata esemokwu DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(ndabara dị na koodu)_       | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Ọnụọgụ ndenye mmefu kachasị a ga-edobe na buffer tupu a manye flush. Welie ya na nrụnye nwere QPS dị elu; belata ya mgbe idobe ojiji ebe nchekwa n'oke dị mkpa karịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(edoghị)_                    | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Tọọ ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` iji wepụta ndekọ debug `[ProxyFetch]` n'ụzọ relay Vercel. A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara ka ọ ghara ikpughe ntụnye gbasara ụzọ ngafe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_BINARY_PATH`         | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/sync.ts`        | Ụzọ zuru oke nke binary proxy core mpaghara (faịlụ aha ya bụ `sing-box` / `mihomo`) nke na-enyocha nhazi core emepụtara tupu ọ dochie faịlụ anabatara. Mgbe edoghị ya, ọ na-ede naanị `<path>.generated` n'akụkụ faịlụ anabatara. Maka gburugburu host naanị: anaghị echekwa ya na database ma ọ bụ nabata ya site na management API, n'ihi na a na-agba binary ahụ ọsọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_RELOAD_COMMAND`      | _(edoghị)_                    | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/reload.ts`      | Otu proxy core mpaghara si ebugharịgharị mgbe edochiri nhazi ekwenyesiri ike: ndepụta argument JSON a na-agba ọsọ na-enweghị shell, ma ọ bụ kpọmkwem `external` mgbe onye nlekota dị n'èzí na-etinye faịlụ ahụ n'ọrụ. Anaghị echekwa ya na database ma ọ bụ kpughee ya site na API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                       | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Tọọ ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` ma ọ bụ `"1"` iji tinye IP ndị client/egress na prefix akaụntụ n'ahịrị process-log zuru ezu `[ProxyEgress]`. A na-edobe ya ka ọ bụrụ OFF na ndabara ka process log ghara ikpughe IP ma ọ bụ prefix akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(edoghị)_                    | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Tọọ ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji bipụta nyocha oge nke arịrịọ ọ bụla (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) site na iwu quota CLI gaa na stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(akpaghị aka)_               | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Ụzọ akọwapụtara nke nyocha ahụike konteena na-enyocha. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, nyocha ahụ na-ewepụta ya site na `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; idobe ya na-eme ka e jiri ọzọ endpoint nlekota miri emi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(edoghị ya)_                 | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ uru ọ bụla na-abụghị ihe efu iji wepụta nchọpụta nsogbu `[omniroute completion]` site n’ụzọ cache mmecha-shell nke CLI (ịgụ/imegharị/dee). Agbanyụghị ya na ndabara — cache ndị ahụ na-ada na-enweghị ozi ka cache na-efu ma ọ bụ mebiri emebi ghara imebi mmecha site na taabụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (24h)              | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Ogologo oge kachasị maka ịnwagharị ihe ọ bụla dị n’otu batch (ms). A na-akara ihe ndị gafere oge a dị ka ndị dara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oge nchere ntọala (ms) maka nkwụsị na-abawanye n’usoro exponential mgbe a na-anwagharị ihe dị na batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (1h)                | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oke kachasị (ms) maka nkwụsị na-abawanye n’usoro exponential n’etiti ịnwagharị ihe dị na batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Ọnụọgụ kachasị nke batch a ga-ahazi n’otu oge. Welie ya iji mụbaa ọsọ nhazi; debe ya ala iji zere oke arịrịọ n’otu oge nke ga-akpalite mmachi ọsọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Tupu ịgbanwe nchekwa data na-adịgide adịgide nke dịbu, onye na-agba migration na-ebipụta snapshot zuru ezu,
> nke adreesị ya dabere n’ọdịnaya, n’okpuru `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Mbipụta chọrọ sistemụ faịlụ
> na-akwado hard link ndị nọ n’otu sistemụ faịlụ, ndị na-adịghị edegharị faịlụ dịbu, tinyere mmekọrịta faịlụ na-adịgide adịgide. Ndị nnabata POSIX chọkwara
> mmekọrịta ndekọ; na Windows, Node nwere ike jụ handle ndekọ, ya mere OmniRoute na-edecha faịlụ
> e bipụtara ma na-ewere mmekọrịta ntinye-ndekọ dịka ihe a ga-eme dịka o kwere mee.
> Ọ bụrụ na `DATA_DIR` e jikọtara enweghị ike inye nkwa ndị ahụ, mmalite ga-akwụsị n’enweghị itinye
> migration. Bugharịa `DATA_DIR` gaa na volume nwere ikike ndị ahụ; ejila
> `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` gafere nchekwa migration.

### Ọnọdụ ojiji

| Ọnọdụ                            | Nhazi                                                                             |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Mmepe mpaghara**               | Hapụ ntọala ndabara niile. DB dị na `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.                  |
| **Docker**                       | `DATA_DIR=/data` + jikọta volume na `/data`.                                      |
| **E zoro ezo mgbe echekwara ya** | Tọọ `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + debe ndabere nke igodo ahụ! Ịtụfu ya = ịtụfu data. |
| **CI/Nnwale**                    | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — nke nwa oge, izochi ya adịghị mkpa.              |

---

## 3. Netwọkụ & Ọdụ Ụzọ

| Mgbanwe                                     | Ndabara                                         | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                                         | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Ọdụ ụzọ bụ isi maka UI Dashboard na njedebe API niile (ọnọdụ otu ọdụ ụzọ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(ọ tọgbọ chakoo = mgbọrọgwụ)_                  | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | Obere ụzọ URL maka ịnye OmniRoute site n'azụ proxy ntụgharị (ọ na-ahazi `basePath` nke Next.js; ntụgharị auth na-eburu basePath n'uche). Dịka ọmụmaatụ, `/omniroute`. Na Docker, a na-etinye uru ahụ n'ime ya n'oge `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); image mgbọrọgwụ ndị e wuru tupu oge eruo nwere ike itinye uru runtime dị iche otu ugboro mgbe container malitere tupu Next.js amalite. Tọọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ origin ọha nke gụnyere otu obere ụzọ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(ọ tọgbọ chakoo = mgbọrọgwụ)_                  | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Oyiri `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` nke ihe nchọgharị na-ahụ, nke a na-etinye n'ime build n'oge a na-ewu ya ka ngosipụta endpoint nke dashboard wee gosi `https://host/omniroute/v1` kama `https://host/v1`. Ọ na-eji `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya. Wughachi ya mgbe ị gbanwechara ya (`basePath` nke Next bụ nke oge build).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(edobeghị = agaghị etinye ya na frame ma ọlị)_ | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Ịhọrọ ikwe ka etinye ibe HTML n'ime iframe. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, route ọ bụla ga-eji `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `vscode` iji jiri `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` na-enweghị `X-Frame-Options` nye ibe ndị ahụ (dashboard, nbanye, docs, ibe mbata), ka VS Code Simple Browser nwee ike igosipụta ha (ọnọdụ `dashboardOpen: "editor"` nke OmniCopilot). Akụkụ API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, aha nnọchi ndị dị n'ogo root) ga-anọgide na-eji header ndị siri ike n'agbanyeghị nke a. Naanị `vscode` ka a na-amata — `1`/`true` anaghị eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ. N'oge build: mee build ọzọ mgbe ị gbanwechara ya (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` maka image; ịtọ ya na nrụnye e wuru tupu oge ahụ agaghị enwe mmetụta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe edobere ya, ọ na-enye proxy API `/v1/*` (gbakwunyere Gemini-native `/v1beta/*`) na port a dị iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                                       | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Adreesị bind maka port API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(edobeghị)_                                    | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe edobere ya, ọ na-enye UI Dashboard na port a dị iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(agbanyụrụ)_                               | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Na-amachi njikọ nkata ndị na-arụ ọrụ n'otu oge; arịrịọ gafere oke ahụ ga-enweta `503` nwere `Retry-After`. Integer dị mma na-eme ka nchedo ahụ rụọ ọrụ; ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya ma ọ bụ na ọ bụ `0`, ọ ga-agbanyụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(edoghị ya)_                                   | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | ID kwụsiri ike ma bụrụ nke pụrụ iche maka gateway a mgbe a na-ejikọta instance OmniRoute n'usoro. Ọ na-eme ka nyocha loop nke peer na-abata rụọ ọrụ. Mkpụrụedemede ndị a kwere: mkpụrụedemede, ọnụọgụ, `.`, `_`, `:`, na `-`; kacha mkpụrụedemede 64.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(edoghị ya)_                                   | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | URL ntọala OmniRoute ndị e ji rikoma kewaa nke nwere ike ịnata `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. Ọ bụ naanị URL upstream ndị e tinyere kpọmkwem na ndepụta ikike ga-anata metadata peer; ndị provider ndị ọzọ niile agaghị agbanwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                             | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Ọnụọgụ kachasị elu nke instance OmniRoute ndị a gara na mbụ nke a ga-anabata na request ejikọrọ n'usoro (`1`-`32`). Instance ndị e megharịrị ma ọ bụ budget agwụla ga-eweghachi HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT`                       | `20130`                                         | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port e bipụtara n'akụkụ host maka Dashboard n'ụdị production Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROD_API_PORT`                             | `20131`                                         | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port e bipụtara n'akụkụ host maka API n'ụdị production Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PORT`                            | _(ahazighị)_                                    | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Ọ na-ebute ụzọ karịa `PORT` mgbe a na-agba ya n'ime Electron ma ọ bụ ihe mkpuchi ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                                         | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri maka sava nlekota ozugbo nke WebSocket.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                     | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Adreesị njikọ maka sava WebSocket dị ndụ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0.0.0.0` iji mee ka a nweta ya na LAN (haziekwa `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`). Njikọ na-eji kuki nnọkọ dashboard ma ọ bụ igodo Bearer API nwere ikike njikwa (`manage`/`admin`) eme nyocha njirimara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(ahazighị)_                                    | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Isi mmalite ndị ọzọ e ji rikoma kewaa, ndị enyere ohere imepe WebSocket dị ndụ. A na-ekwekwa isi mmalite dashboard loopback na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(ahazighị)_                                    | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Aha ndị ọbịa ndị ọzọ e ji rikoma kewaa, ndị enyere ohere maka isi mmalite WebSocket dị ndụ. N'adịghị ka `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (URL zuru ezu nke isi mmalite), ọ na-adaba naanị na akụkụ host — nke bara uru maka nhazi LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(edoghị ya)_                                   | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL ọha maka WebSocket nke dashboard dị ndụ (n'akụkụ ihe nchọgharị). Tọọ ya mgbe reverse proxy ma ọ bụ Cloudflare Tunnel nọ n'ihu sava WS (dịka `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); ihe nchọgharị ga-ejikọ ebe ahụ kama ijikọ na `ws://hostname:20132`. A na-ejikwa akụkụ pathname dị ka ụzọ nkwalite WebSocket (ndabara: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                                          | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` na `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` ma ọ bụ `false` iji gbanyụọ sava WebSocket nke oge-ozugbo (agbanyere ya na ndabara, ma kegide ya na loopback).
