Nke a bụ mgba ọkụ CI/harness nke na-agbanyụọ skripụ enyemaka WebSocket dị ndụ nke na-arụ ọrụ naanị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                            | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Oke ọsọ relay maka (token, IP) ọ bụla, arịrịọ kwa nkeji. Ọ dị na ebe nchekwa, maka instance ọ bụla. `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-adịghị mma na-agbanyụọ ọnụ ụzọ mgbochi nke akụkụ IP (oke DB maka token ọ bụla ka na-emetụta).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                                    | Isi Next.js                                                              | Na-achịkwa oke nkọwa ndekọ, caching, ikpughe nkọwa njehie, na nkwalite arụmọrụ Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | _(etinyeghị)_ (nhọrọ build na-akpaghị aka)      | `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs`                                  | Maka `npm run build`, uru a na-etinyeghị ma ọ bụ tọgbọ chakoo na-ahọrọ webpack mgbe oke memory dị irè (RAM anụ ahụ ma ọ bụ oke cgroup/process dị ala) adịghị karịa **16 GiB**, ma ọ bụ mgbe a pụghị ịchọpụta ya n'enweghị ihe ize ndụ; karịa 16 GiB, a na-ahọrọ Turbopack. `0` etinyere kpọmkwem na-amanye webpack; `1` etinyere kpọmkwem na uru ndị ọzọ na-adịghị tọgbọ chakoo ma na-abụghị efu na-amanye Turbopack. Uru environment tọgbọ chakoo Compose zitere **anaghị** amanye Turbopack. Oke a sitere na ndaghachi azụ memory build nke 15.6 GiB (#18184); a naghị eme benchmark nke builds na host nwere 24/32 GiB. Webpack production na-awụfe cache na-adịgide adịgide ya ozugbo dịka ndabara (lee `OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE` n'okpuru; #18141), ọ na-emekwa build nwere elu RSS dị ala nke ukwuu karịa ụzọ Turbopack ugbu a. Development na-edebe omume bundler dị ugbu a. **Docker builds na-eji `0` (webpack) dịka ndabara ugbu a**; jiri `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=1` iji gbanye Turbopack kpọmkwem. Lee `docs/guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE`             | _(edoghị ya)_                                   | `next.config.mjs`                                                        | Owuwu production webpack (`OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=0`) na-amafe cache filesystem na-adịgide adịgide nke webpack belụsọ ma nke a bụrụ `1`. Ide cache ahụ na-agbakwunye ihe dịka 2.5 GB na oke ebe nchekwa owuwu mgbe heap cap bụ 6144 MB, owuwu dị ọcha anaghị agụghachi ya. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji debe `.build/next/cache/webpack` maka owuwu ndị a na-emeghachi ngwa ngwa n'igwe nwere RAM ga-ezu; owuwu Docker na-anabata ya dịka `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_WEBPACK_BUILD_CACHE=1` (usoro builder nke `Dockerfile` na `Dockerfile.bun`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(edoghị ya)_                                   | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji wụfee nyocha ahụike nke iguzosi ike n'ezi ihe SQLite mgbe mmalite. Ọ bara uru maka mmalite ngwa ngwa n'ebe ọdụ data buru ibu dị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(edoghị ya)_                                   | systemd (usoro sd_notify)                                                | systemd na-edobe ya mgbe usoro ahụ na-arụ ọrụ n'okpuru unit ọrụ nwere njikọ sd_notify; OmniRoute na-agụ ya (lee `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) iji zipụ ọkwa READY/WATCHDOG. Onye ọrụ ekwesịghị idebe ya ma ọlị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(edoghị ya)_                                   | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji gbanyụọ systemd sd_notify (Type=notify / WatchdogSec=) ọbụlagodi mgbe ọ na-arụ ọrụ n'okpuru unit systemd. Onye na-ezipụ ọkwa anaghị arụ ihe ọ bụla na mpụga systemd n'agbanyeghị ọnọdụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `3600000`                                       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Oge etiti (ms) maka onye nhazi nyocha ahụike nzere na-arụ ọrụ n'azụ. Nke kacha nta: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                                        | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) maka ọnọdụ ahụike nzere echekwara na cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                                         | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Tọọ ka ọ bụrụ `1` ma ọ bụ `true` iji gbanyụọ nnwale njikọ ndị na-eweta ọrụ nke na-eme kwa oge n'azụ. A na-ewepụ ndị na-eweta ọchụchọ (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS dị na `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, dịka `tavily-search`) mgbe niile na nyocha a — “nkwado” ha bụ ajụjụ upstream n'ezie a na-akwụ ụgwọ maka ya, ya mere anaghị enyocha ahụike ha site na timer (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `DEEP_HEALTH_CHECK_ENABLED`                 | `0`                                             | `src/app/api/monitoring/health/route.ts`                                 | Tọọ ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji kwe ka onye na-akpọ nwere nkwenye rịọ `/api/monitoring/health?deep=1`, nke na-ewere otu nlele nke elu completions n'otu TTL. Ndị na-akpọ na-enweghị njirimara anaghị ebute probe ahụ mgbe ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS`   | `5000`                                          | `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`                                                  | Oke nkwụsị (ms) maka boot event-loop recorder: freeze nke toro ogologo karịa nke a na-edekọ otu ahịrị `event loop stalled` mgbe ọrụ maliteghachiri, tinyere ọnụọgụ ebe nchekwa na ọrụ ndị ka na-aga, ma na-abawanye counter/maximum `eventLoopStall` na `/api/monitoring/health`. Tọọ ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya; uru a na-apụghị ịgụ ma ọ bụ nke na-erughị efu ga-alaghachi na `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                                       | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Adreesị bind maka sava dev/start nke Next.js. Mgbe edobere ya, ọ na-anọchi ndabara `0.0.0.0`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                                     | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adreesị bind nke Playwright runner na-eji mgbe ọ na-ebido Next.js. Ndabara ya bụ `127.0.0.1` maka nnwale hermetic. **Ejila ya maka `omniroute serve`** — jiri `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` kama (shell POSIX na-edobe `HOSTNAME` ka ọ bụrụ aha igwe na-akpaghị aka; `.env` enweghị ike ịnọchi ya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                                       | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Adreesị njikọ maka `omniroute serve`. Ọ na-egbochi esemokwu na mgbanwe POSIX shell `HOSTNAME` (nke bash/zsh na-edobe mgbe niile ka ọ bụrụ aha igwe ahụ). Ọ na-eji `0.0.0.0` ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

### Ụdị Ọdụ

```
┌────────────────────────────── Otu Ọdụ (ndabara) ────────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Ogwe njikwa: http://localhost:20128                                      │
│  → API:          http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions                  │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌────────────────────────────── Ọdụ E Kewara Ekewa ───────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Ogwe njikwa: http://localhost:20128                                      │
│  → API:          http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions                   │
│  Ojiji: Mee ka LAN nweta API ma gbochie Ogwe njikwa ka ọ nọrọ na localhost. │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌──────────────────────────── Mmepụta Docker ─────────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Na-ejikọta ọdụ container na ọdụ host n'ime docker-compose.prod.yml.     │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Nchekwa & Nyocha njirimara

| Variable                                    | Default                                                                           | Source File                                              | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                             | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Nnu jikọtara na ihe nchọpụta ngwaike maka ịmepụta akara njirimara igwe. Gbanwee ya kwa nkenye iji kewapụ nkenye dị iche iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(unset = random per-install salt persisted at `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`)_ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | Nnu HMAC maka ịmepụta token nyocha CLI nke mpaghara. Ịtọlite uru a na-atụgharị token CLI niile dị n’igwe ahụ, ọ na-ebukwa ụzọ mgbe niile karịa nnu echekwara. Lee `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                           | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Na-edobe ọkọlọtọ `Secure` n’elu kuki nnọkọ. **Ọ GHAGHỊ ịbụ `true`** mgbe a na-agba ya n’azụ HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                           | API middleware                                           | Mgbe enyere ya aka, onye ahịa API proxy chọrọ nzere anabatara maka `/api/v1/*` na `/v1/*`, gụnyere `GET /v1/models` n'ihu onye na-elekọta ya. Asambodo na-agbaso amụma API onye ahịa ( igodo API ma ọ bụ nnọkọ anabatara anabatara). Katalọgụ ahụ nwekwara nlele `requireAuthForModels` onye na-ahụ maka ya, nke enweghị ike ịgafe proxy. Na ọkọlọtọ zuru ụwa ọnụ kwụsịrị, katalọgụ ka nwere ike ịlaghachi `401`; na nzaghachi naanị adịghị egosi na a na-echebe inference. Lee `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695). Nabata `true`, `1` ma ọ bụ `yes`, na-eleghara ikpe na oghere ndị gbara ya gburugburu; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (gụnyere `on`) agbanyụrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                           | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Na-enye ohere igosi ụkpụrụ igodo API zuru ezu na UI Dashboard. Enwere ike ịhazi ya site na Ọkọlọtọ Njirimara Dashboard; ọ bụ ihe egwu nchekwa n’ihe atụ nkekọrịta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(efu)_                                                                           | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | NJirimara igodo API ndị e kewara site na rikoma ndị na-agafe ndekọ arịrịọ (n’ihi nrube isi GDPR).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(edobeghị = enweghị oke)_                                                        | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Mmefu arịrịọ kwa ụbọchị nke ndabara a na-etinye n’isi igodo API ndị kọlụm `rate_limits` ha bụ null. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya ma ọ bụ ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo: enweghị oke zoro ezo (#2289, #11017). `0` pụtara otu ihe ahụ (enweghị oke). Ọnụọgụ zuru ezu dị mma N na-eme ka ọ bụrụ N kwa ụbọchị, 5N kwa izu, yana 20N kwa ọnwa. Uru na-ezughị oke ma na-abụghị efu na-alaghachi na windo ochie nke 1000 kwa ụbọchị, 5000 kwa izu, na 20000 kwa ọnwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                                | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | Ndabara maka ntọala a na-ahụ anya `maxBodySizeMb`; ntọala echekwara na-ebute ụzọ. `/api/v1/chat/completions` na `/api/v1/responses` na-ejigide ndabara 50 MB ruo mgbe echekwara oke; mgbe echekwara oke, ọ na-anọchi ya, ma ọ bụrụ na ọ na-arị elu ma ọ bụ na-agbada, maka nchekwa nkụnye eji isi mee na maka nnabata chat dabere na byte ndị dị adị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`           | `262144` (256 KB)                                                                 | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ahụ arịrịọ n’ezie ndị ruru ma ọ bụ gafere oke a na-enweta atomic heavyweight admission lease nke dị n’ime process tupu nyocha JSON (ụzọ BYTE, gụnyere `POST /v1/responses`). Otu mgbapụ healthy-headroom nke [#10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) ahụ dị maka structure-heavy ka dịkwa; mana `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` na [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) ka na-ekpebi oke ya, ka [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) ghara ịlaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                                | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Oke siri ike nke ụzọ chat na-enyocha megide byte ndị a gụrụ n’oge nnabata nwere oke, gụnyere arịrịọ ndị `Content-Length` ha na-efu, adịghị mma, ma ọ bụ na-enye ozi na-abụghị eziokwu; ihe karịrị oke na-enweta `413`. `maxBodySizeMb` echekwara na-anọchi ndabara 50 MB; mgbe edobere mgbanwe a, ọ na-akpachapụkwa uru echekwara ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(akọpụtaghị — enweghị oke ọnụọgụ arịrịọ)_                                        | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503-fanout:** oke COUNT arịrịọ ochie a na-arụ ọrụ naanị mgbe edobere ya hoo haa. Mgbe a hapụrụ ya ka ọ ghara ịdị (ndabara), nnabata nkata dị arọ na-adabere kama na `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` — mmefu ego BYTE a na-emepụta na akpaghị aka, nke sitere n'oke ebe nchekwa usoro ahụ nwere n'ezie n'ime **otu usoro** (otu V8 heap). Arịrịọ `/v1/responses` abụọ jikọtara ọnụ, nke ọ bụla nwere ihe dị ka token 750k, na-akwụsị ihe dị ka 12 Gi heaps (#7849) — nke a bụ ịdọ aka ná ntị gbasara **mmefu ego ebe nchekwa**, ọ bụghị oke ngwaahịa siri ike nke 2. Usoro dị mma (heap dị n'okpuru nha shed) nwere ike ịnabata ọtụtụ `/v1/responses` dị ogologo na-aga n'otu oge site na `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Ọtụtụ ndị ahịa SSE dị ogologo (40–50) bụ okwu heap + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, ọ bụghị “max 2”. Ịkwalite nke a n'enweghị nyocha iji “jiri host ahụ” na-eweghachite #7849. Jiri **heaps** mụbaa ọnụ ọgụgụ ahụ na **N `DATA_DIR`s nọọrọ onwe ha** (#11024); etinyela `replicas>1` na otu faịlụ SQLite.                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(a na-emepụta na akpaghị aka)_                                                   | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503-fanout:** uru a na-eji dochie mmefu ego byte nnabata nke a na-emepụta na akpaghị aka (25% nke oke ebe nchekwa V8/cgroup kacha nta, kewara site na mmụba nwa oge nke 8x). Uru ndị a rụpụtara na ndị e nyere hoo haa na-anọ n'etiti 8 MiB–2 GiB. Ahụ karịrị mmefu ego dị irè na-ada ozugbo na `413 body_exceeds_budget`; asọmpi dị n'etiti ahụ ndị nwere ike ijere ozi n'otu n'otu ka na-enye ohere ịnwale ọzọ site na `503`. Ndị ahịa SSE dị ogologo 40–50 na-aga n'otu oge bụ mmefu ego a + heap, ọ bụghị “max 2” siri ike. Gụọ `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` na `/api/monitoring/health` tupu ịhazigharị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

A na-atụfu arịrịọ dị arọ na-aga n'otu oge nke gafere `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` naanị site na `503` nke a pụrụ ịnwale ọzọ mgbe heap dịkwa na ma ọ bụ karịa oke a; na heap dị mma, a na-anabata ya site na healthy-headroom kama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

Enweghị oke a, arịrịọ ọ bụla na-arụsi ọrụ ike mana heap ya dị mma na-agafe nnabata n'enweghị elu-oke. Mgbe leases a na-arụ ọrụ n'otu oge site n'ụzọ healthy-heap ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a, arịrịọ ndị ọzọ na-arụsi ọrụ ike na-agafe n'ụzọ SAME bounded-wait/shed ahụ a na-eji mgbe enwere nrụgide heap n'ezie. `0` na-ewepụ ụzọ ịgafe a kpamkpam.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`     | `false`                                                                           | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Ọ na-agbanyụ nlele host nke outbound URL guard, tinyere mgbochi cloud-metadata (169.254.0.0/16 niile, metadata.google.internal), n’ụzọ provider ndị na-agbaso guard mode: nkwado URL, nchọpụta model, base URL nke provider-node (gụnyere remote rerank nodes), na ule proxy-fallback, tinyere ụzọ outbound ndị ọzọ metụtara provider dịka remote image/media fetch. Ọ na-ahapụkwa private webhook targets, ewezuga cloud-metadata/link-local endpoints, ndị ka na-anọgide na-egbochi ọbụlagodi mgbe flag a dị na ọnọdụ agbanyere. Arịrịọ chat ndị `BaseExecutor.execute()` zipụrụ na-aga n’ihu na nlele cloud-metadata ha. Ndị provider mpaghara na-arụ ọrụ site na ndabara: `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` (ndabara `true`) na-ahapụrịrị local na LAN URLs na validation, discovery, na provider-node paths; ule proxy-fallback na private webhook targets na-ele naanị flag a anya ma na-egbochi local/LAN hosts mgbe ọ dị na ọnọdụ agbanyụghị. Ọ na-emeri `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS=false`. Uru e chekwara site na dashboard toggle na-ebute ụzọ karịa variable a. |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                            | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Kwe ka provider URLs dị na adreesị local/private (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, private ranges) maka provider validation, nchọpụta model, chat, na ụzọ outbound ndị ọzọ metụtara provider nke guard mode na-achịkwa. **Ndabara `true`** (local-first): guard ahụ na-egbochi cloud-metadata endpoints — 169.254.0.0/16 niile tinyere metadata hostnames ndị a maara (metadata.google.internal, metadata.goog, Alibaba/Azure/Oracle IMDS, fd00:ec2::254). Tọọ ya `false` maka public-only mode, nke na-egbochikwa private na loopback hosts. Guard ahụ na-enyocha hostname ma ọ bụ IP literal dịka e dere ya, tupu DNS lookup ọ bụla; na public-only mode, safe-fetch wrapper na-edozikwa hostname ahụ ma jụ azịza private ọ bụla. `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` na-agbanyụ nlele ndị a n’ụzọ ndị ahịrị ya depụtara. Built-in local providers, dịka LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, na Triton, na-amapụ nlele host niile maka key validation na chat. Uru e chekwara site na dashboard toggle na-ebute ụzọ karịa variable a. (#5066)                                       |

A na-emechi ya na ndabara — iziga ọdịyo na host dịpụrụ adịpụ na-agbanwe njirimara egress, ya mere ọ ga-abụrịrị mkpebi doro anya nke onye na-arụ ọrụ. A na-anabata ọnụ loopback/ndị nkeonwe (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) mgbe niile, ọ dịghịkwa emetụta ha. A na-emechi ya na ndabara — iziga arịrịọ na host dịpụrụ adịpụ na-agbanwe njirimara egress, ya mere ọ ga-abụrịrị mkpebi doro anya nke onye na-arụ ọrụ. A na-anabata ọnụ loopback (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) mgbe niile, ọ dịghịkwa emetụta ha. URL ntọala nke ọnụ dịpụrụ adịpụ ga-agafekwa iwu URL outbound nke provider (`OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` / `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_PROVIDER_NODE_HOSTS`       | _(efu)_                                                                           | `src/shared/network/localNodeHosts.ts`                   | Aha host (nkewapụtara site na rikoma ma ọ bụ oghere) nke ntụgharị ọnụ provider na-ewere dị ka nke mpaghara — ọ na-abụkarị aha ọrụ Docker/Compose nke reranker, TEI ma ọ bụ audio sidecar dị na netwọk Docker nke OmniRoute n'onwe ya (`http://reranker:8080/v1`), ka ọnụ ahụ ghara ịchọ adreesị IP akpa ejiri aka dee. Host ndị e depụtara tozuru maka `/v1/rerank`, `/v1/audio/*` na nlele ahụike nke mpaghara, n'enweghị ịgbanye `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` / `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` maka ọnụ dịpụrụ adịpụ ọ bụla, a na-akpọkwa ha ozugbo — ọ dịghị mgbe site na `HTTP(S)_PROXY` ma ọ bụ proxy e tinyere n'ọnọdụ njikọ. Ha ka na-eziga igodo API nke njikọ ahụ. A na-anabata naanị aha nwere label otu na aha `.internal`/`.local`; a na-eleghara public FQDN, IP literals na aha metadata igwe ojii anya, tinyekwara ịdọ aka ná ntị. (#14635)                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`     | `false`                                                                           | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Mgbe agbanyere OIDC, gbanyụọ nbanye paswọọdụ ka ndị ọrụ nwee ike ịkwado njirimara naanị site na OIDC Single Sign-On. A na-anabatakwa aha mkpirisi `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`; ọkọlọtọ Feature nke Dashboard nwere otu igodo ahụ na-ebute ụzọ. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`               | `false`                                                                           | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Aha dị mfe nke `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN` (#10889).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_MFA`                     | `false`                                                                           | `src/server/auth/dashboardMfa.ts`                        | Nweghachite ngwaọrụ furu efu maka nbanye dashboard TOTP nwere nkwenye ihe abụọ nke onye ọrụ họọrọ. Mgbe `true` dị, a na-awụpụ ihe nke abụọ naanị maka nbanye sitere na host ahụ n'onwe ya (loopback peer na loopback `Host`); nbanye dịpụrụ adịpụ na `POST /api/cli/connect` sitere na origins ndị ọzọ ka chọrọ koodu ahụ. Banye na mpaghara, gbanyụọ nkwenye ihe abụọ n'okpuru Settings → Security (enwere ike ịhapụ koodu ahụ n'efu mgbe nke a debere, ya mere enwere ike iwepụ ihe na-adịghịzi akwado; a ka chọrọ okwuntughe), wee wepụ ya. TOTP na-ekpuchi nbanye okwuntughe na `omniroute connect` naanị — nbanye OIDC/SSO dabere na nkwenye ihe abụọ nke onye na-enye njirimara n'onwe ya. Lee [AUTHZ_GUIDE](../architecture/AUTHZ_GUIDE.md#optional-totp-two-factor-login-16619).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |

### Ndepụta Nyocha Maka Ịkwalite Nchekwa

```bash
# Opekempe nchekwa mmepụta:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Chọrọ HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Nyochaa oku proxy niile
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Adịghị kpughee igodo na UI
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # Oke 5 MB
```

---

## 5. Nhichapụ Ihe Ntinye & Nchedo PII

OmniRoute na-enye nchedo nwere ọkwa abụọ: nyocha ntụnye ọjọọ n'akụkụ arịrịọ na iwepụ PII n'akụkụ nzaghachi.

> **⚠️ Oke ya:** Usoro nchedo ndị a bụ nchọpụta _heuristic nke a na-agba mbọ ime nke ọma_, ọ bụghị firewall zuru oke megide ntụnye ọjọọ na prompt ma ọ bụ sistemụ PII DLP. Ha nwere ike iwepụta nchọpụta ụgha (dịka ịkọpụta prompt persona/RPG na-enweghị nsogbu dịka nke ọjọọ) na enweghị nchọpụta mgbe ihe ọjọọ dị (dịka leetspeak, oghere dị iche iche, na usoro na-abụghị Bekee). Naanị ha ezughị maka irube isi n'ụkpụrụ. Hazie mode ndị ahụ ma nwalee ha megide traffic gị tupu ịdabere na ha.

### N'akụkụ Arịrịọ: Nchedo Megide Prompt Injection

| Variable                          | Nke Ndabara  | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                |
| --------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Na-eme ka nyocha ozi ndị na-abata maka usoro prompt injection rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                               |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Iwu injection: `warn` = dekọọ naanị, `block` = jụ arịrịọ site na 400. `redact` ochie **anaghị** ewepụ ederede injection; jiri `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` maka idegharị PII dị n'arịrịọ. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(ahazighị)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                                        |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`       | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Ogo ihe ize ndụ kacha nta nke `MODE=block` ga-ajụ: `high` (ndabara), `medium`, ma ọ bụ `low`. A na-elele usoro `medium` naanị ma ọ bụrụ na e belataghị ya.                           |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(ahazighị)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                             |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, zoo PII n'arịrịọ ndị na-abata (ọ naghị adabere na mode injection).                                                                                                 |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Zoo usoro API-key / secret-token ndị a ma ama na payload arịrịọ/nzaghachi. A ga-ahọrọ ya ka ọ rụọ ọrụ; ọ na-eme dịka `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                        |

### N'akụkụ Nzaghachi: Ihe Nhichapụ PII

| Variable                         | Nke Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite         | Nkọwa                                                                        |
| -------------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`     | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Nyochaa nzaghachi LLM maka PII gbapụrụ tupu iziga ya nye client.             |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact`    | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = zoo PII, `warn` = dekọọ naanị, `block` = tụfuo nzaghachi ahụ dum. |

### Ihe Nhichapụ Context nke Tokenized-Route na VS Code

| Variable                            | Nke Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`         | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Na-ewepụ context implicit nke active-editor (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) n'arịrịọ `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` ma zoo ọdịnaya faịlụ nwere mmetụta dị elu ndị e tinyere n'ụzọ doro anya. Ọ dị nchebe na ndabara; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya. |

### Ọnọdụ Ojiji

| Ọnọdụ Ojiji                    | Nhazi                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Irube isi n'ụkpụrụ ụlọ ọrụ** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (igbochi injection + izochi PII dị n'arịrịọ/nzaghachi; mode ndị ahụ anaghị adabere ibe ha) |
| **Nlekota naanị**              | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — na-edekọ mana ọ naghị egbochi mgbe ọ bụla                                                                                                                 |
| **Ojiji nkeonwe**              | Hapụ ha niile ka ha ghara ịrụ ọrụ — enweghị overhead ọ bụla                                                                                                                                                             |

---

## 6. Amụma Ngwaọrụ na Ịkwọ Ụzọ

| Mgbanwe                                                     | Ndabara                      | Faịlụ Isi                                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                   | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                           | Na-achịkwa ohere LLM nwere iji ngwaọrụ/ịkpọ ọrụ. `allowlist` = naanị ngwaọrụ ndị e depụtara, `denylist` = ngwaọrụ niile ewezuga ndị e depụtara, `disabled` = enweghị mmachibido.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json` | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`               | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ JSON nke iwu nhazi payload (mmezi upstream dabere n’ụdị model/protocol).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                       | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`               | Oge etiti nbugharị (ms) maka ibugharị faịlụ iwu payload ozugbo. Nke kacha nta bụ `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                      | Nhọrọ ịgbalite: na-esi n’aka ndị ahịa Claude Code duru model ID `claude-*` na-enweghị prefix site na akaụntụ Claude Code OAuth kama ịrịọ prefix onye na-enye ọrụ. Prefix onye na-enye ọrụ doro anya ka na-enwe mmeri. A pụkwara ịhazi ya site na toggle dashboard dị na ibe onye na-enye ọrụ Claude.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDACT_THINKING`                                 | `true`                       | `open-sse/executors/base/redactThinkingBeta.ts`   | Tọọ `false` iji kwụsị OmniRoute ịgbakwunye Anthropic beta `redact-thinking-2026-02-12` n’onwe ya (thinking blocks nwere naanị mbinye aka efu). Arịrịọ nwere `thinking.display: "summarized"` na-amapụ ya n’agbanyeghị; beta redact onye ahịa kparịtara ma ọ bụ opt-in `requestDefaults.redactThinking` dakọtara na CC na-eme ka ọ dịgide. Arịrịọ Claude OAuth na-edobe beta ahụ (ọ bụ akụkụ nke akara mkpịsị aka Claude Code OAuth) belụsọ ma feature flag `CLAUDE_OAUTH_SUMMARIZED_THINKING_UNREDACTED`, nke ndabara ya bụ gbanyụọ, dị n’ọrụ.                                              |
| `BARE_MODEL_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_LEGACY`                        | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                      | Àgwà ochie `resolveBareModelToConnectionDefault` (#4825): jiri `defaultModel` nke njikọ ahọpụtara dochie aha model ọ bụla na-enweghị prefix. Ọ na-adịkwaghị n’ọrụ na ndabara; naanị `auto` ma ọ bụ model efu ka a na-edochi, ọ na-edekwa ịdọ aka ná ntị otu ugboro maka ụzọ abụọ ọ bụla mgbe ọ dị n’ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                          | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`                | Oke concurrent kwa model maka combo round-robin (#9100). A kpara semaphore nke combo round-robin ike na arịrịọ concurrent 3 kwa model, na-enweghị override, nke na-eme ka okporo ụzọ nwere concurrency dị elu chere n’azụ oke ahụ. A kwadoro ya ka ọ bụrụ `>= 1`, ma kpachie ya na `<= 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENTIC_STALL_FAILOVER`                          | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/combo/agenticStallFailover.ts` | Nhọrọ ịgbalite: tọọ ya na `1` (ma ọ bụ `true`) ka onye otu combo nke zara ntụgharị nsonaazụ-tool site na `finish: stop` + nkọwa + oku-tool efu wee gafee gaa na ebumnuche na-esote (atụmatụ priority-loop, round-robin na pinned sessions). Ọ bụrụ na ọ nweghị ebumnuche na-arụpụta nke ka mma, a ga-eweghachi azịza ahụ kwụsịrị kama njehie, a gaghị akwụkwa ụgwọ maka provider breaker ma ọ bụ model lockout. A na-ejide ntụgharị ndị a na-ezipụ site na stream n’aka onye ahịa ruo mgbe a kwụsịrị ikpe ahụ. A naghị ekewa arịrịọ na-eji ọdịdị `input` nke Responses API. Ndabara bụ OFF. |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                      | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`                   | Nhọrọ dị ize ndụ ịgbalite nke na-amapụ nlele mpaghara OmniRoute maka context-window / max-input-token maka arịrịọ kpọmkwem nke otu model. Ndị na-eweta ọrụ dị n’elu ka na-amanye oke ha n’ezie; mkpakọ prompt na oke output-token nke model n’onwe ya ka na-arụ ọrụ. Usoro ụzọ dị irè bụ Feature Flags DB override > environment variable > default; ọ dịghị mkpa ịmalitegharịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`            | Akwụkwọ YAML `providers:` dị n’ime (RIC-738, D4). Mgbe edobere ya (ma ọ nwere ma ọ bụ enweghị ngọngọ `strategy:`), `/v1/chat/completions` na-atụgharị gaa na unified OpenAI-compatible entry nke self-hosted kama pipeline cloud. Mgbe edoghị ya (ndabara), route ahụ na-aga ozugbo na pipeline cloud dị adị. Lee `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`            | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ YAML nwere otu akwụkwọ `providers:` ahụ dị na `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, maka deployments na-ahọrọ faịlụ kama inline env var. Nke ọ bụla n’ime ha na-arụ ọrụ self-hosted entry.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(unset — open route)_       | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`            | API key nkekọrịta nhọrọ maka unified self-hosted entry (D5 scaffold, echekwara maka usoro per-key quota). Mgbe edobere ya, arịrịọ ga-enwerịrị `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Mgbe edoghị ya: route ahụ ghe oghe, nke kwekọrọ n’ụkpụrụ self-hosted local-provider dị adị (loopback/trusted-network deployment).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`          | Akwụkwọ YAML `strategy:` dị n’ime maka injin routing deterministic (M2/RIC-740, D3) — blacklist/whitelist, cooldown breaker, cost-priority, latency-aware, fallback chain. Ọ na-ekpuchi ngọngọ `strategy:` dị n’ime `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, kwa-key. Lee `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`          | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ YAML nwere otu akwụkwọ `strategy:` ahụ dị na `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, maka deployments na-ahọrọ faịlụ kama inline env var.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(unset)_                    | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts`        | Tọọ `1` iji kwụsị ịnakọta akụkọ mkparịta ụka. `resolveConversationId()` na-eweghachite nsonaazụ a na-adịghị eso tracking tupu ọ gụọ SQLite ma ọ bụ nyochaa akụkọ ozi, a na-ekpuchikwa session IDs ndị client nyere. Ọ dịghị mgbanwe n’otú a si edozi routing-session, a naghị ehichapụkwa records dị adị. Maka deployments na-adịghị eji nlele mkparịta ụka nke dashboard ma chọọ ka tebụl turn kwụsị ịba ụba.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_API_KEY`                                   | _(edoghị atọhapụ)_           | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`    | Igodo API maka atụmatụ router auto-combo `nadir` a na-ahọrọ iji, a na-eji ya mgbe `config.nadir.apiKey` nke combo ahụ n'onwe ya edoghị. Nhọrọ: oku na-enweghị igodo na-adaba na ọkwa anonymous nke Nadir, nke a na-amachibidoro ọnụego ya dabere na IP, ya mere ọ naghị arụ ọrụ n'azụ gateway a na-ekekọrịta. Ọ bụ ihe nwere mmetụta; họrọ ubi nke combo ọ bụla kama mgbe combos na-eji akaụntụ dị iche iche.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_BASE_URL`                                  | `https://api.getnadir.com`   | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`    | Ebe mmalite nke API mkpebi Nadir, a na-eji ya mgbe `config.nadir.baseUrl` nke combo ahụ n'onwe ya edoghị. A chọrọ ya naanị maka Nadir a na-akwado n'onwe ya, nke na-edobe isi prompt n'ime netwọk nke gị. A na-anabata ma wepụ `/v1` nke dị n'azụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                   | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`              | Window kwa izu maka oke ojiji USD kwa igodo API (mmegide arịrịọ, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: izu ahụ na-akwụsị na nrụpụta kwa izu kacha mbụ n'etiti njikọ upstream ndị igodo ahụ nwere ike iru, ma ọ bụ bụrụ ụbọchị 7 na-atụgharị mgbe amaghị nke ọ bụla. `calendar`: izu mpaghara site na Mọnde 00:00 ruo Mọnde na-esote 00:00, otu ihe ahụ maka igodo niile. A na-atụ aro `calendar` mgbe otu ọdọ mmiri nke akaụntụ upstream na-ekekọrịta site n'ọtụtụ igodo nwere mmefu ego nke ọ bụla. Uru na-adịghị mma na-alaghachi na `provider`.                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(timezone usoro)_           | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`              | Mpaghara oge IANA (dịka `Asia/Shanghai`) nke na-akọwa Mọnde 00:00 maka window kwa izu `calendar`. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ma ọ bụ na ọ dịghị mma: timezone nke usoro OmniRoute (the `TZ` nke host ma ọ bụ container, nke na-abụkarị nke edoghị na onyonyo container, ya bụ Coordinated Universal Time). A na-eleghara ya anya na ọnọdụ `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_TOOLS_LIMIT`                                 | `128`                        | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                    | Oke ndabara nke ọnụ ọgụgụ tools a na-ekwe ka ọ dị n'arịrịọ (ndabara OpenAI). Naanị integer dị mma; uru ndị edoghị ma ọ bụ na-adịghị mma na-edobe `128`. Provider nwere oke ama ama/na-arụsi ọrụ ike (`grok-cli` 200, `nvidia` 1536) ka na-etinye oke nke ya — nke a na-emegharị naanị elu oke ndabara. Target ndị dị n'ụdị Claude enweghị oke ndabara (Messages API enweghị nke ọ bụla); oke a mụtara ka na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOOL_OUTPUT_IMAGE_MAX_COUNT`                     | `16`                         | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-responses.ts` | Azịza nye ntụgharị asụsụ nkata: ole onyonyo arụpụta ngwa ọrụ (`function_call_output`, `custom_tool_call_output`, ọrụ `tool`) ezigara ọzọ dị ka akụkụ onyonyo onye ọrụ kwa arịrịọ. A na-edobe nke ọhụrụ ma nke ọ bụla toro eto na-aghọ ebe nchekwa ederede na `tool_call_id` ya. Ọnụ ọgụgụ dị mma naanị; ụkpụrụ adịghị edobe ma ọ bụ ezighi ezi dowe `16`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOOL_OUTPUT_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES`                     | `20971520` (20 MB)           | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-responses.ts` | Ngụkọta nha nke ihe nrụpụta ngwaọrụ URLs ezitere ọzọ kwa arịrịọ n'otu ntụgharị asụsụ ahụ. Foto ndị gara aga na mmefu ego na-ebu ụzọ tufuo nke ochie, yana otu ịdọ aka ná ntị n'otu arịrịọ. Ọnụ ọgụgụ dị mma naanị; ụkpụrụ edobereghị ma ọ bụ ezighi ezi dowe `20971520`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROMPT_EMULATE_TOOLS`                            | _(gbanwuru)_                 | `open-sse/executors/syntxChat.ts`                 | Oku tools nke prompt-emulated nke SYNTX.ai: ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya ma ọ bụ bụrụ `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes`, ọ na-etinye katalọgụ tools mpaghara n'ime prompt; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (dịka `0`) na-amafe itinye ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

## 7. URLs & Mmekọrịta Ígwé ojii

| Mgbanwe                                     | Ndabara                                                   | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `BASE_URL`                                  | `http://localhost:20128`                                  | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL dị n'akụkụ sava nke ọrụ mmekọrịta dị n'ime na-eji akpọ `/api/sync/cloud`. Debe nke a dị ka URL loopback/konteena ọbụlagodi mgbe e ji proxy mee ka ngwa ahụ dịrị ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLOUD_URL`                                 | _(ọ tọgbọ chakoo)_                                        | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL ebe njedebe relay ígwé ojii (atụmatụ premium).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `12000`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Oge nchere HTTP maka arịrịọ mmekọrịta ígwé ojii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                   | `full`                                                    | Nhazi build Webpack                              | Profaịlụ oge build (tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `minimal` iji wepụ modul ndị nwere ikike pụrụ iche kpamkpam na bundle).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                  | _.build/ mmepụta standalone_                              | `scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`          | Ntọgharị oge build maka ndekọ mmepụta standalone nke usoro colocation mgbe build gasịrị na-eji. Ọ bụghị ntọala runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIST_DIR`                        | `dist/` (mgbọrọgwụ repo)                                  | `scripts/build/prepublish.ts`                    | Ntọgharị oge build maka ndekọ ebe usoro mbipụta (`prepublish.ts`) na-ede bundle CLI, kama ide ya na `dist/` dị n'okpuru mgbọrọgwụ repo. A na-ehichapụ ndekọ ahụ tupu build amalite. Ọ bụghị ntọala runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
