| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET`               | _(efu)_                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Nzuzo e kesara nke a na-eji enyocha mbinye aka HMAC-SHA256 nke nzaghachi Cloud Sync.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRETS`              | `false`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji kwe ka endpoint Cloud Sync degharịa ozi nzere mpaghara. Ndabara bụ `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_ENFORCE_SIGNATURE`    | `false`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji jụ nzaghachi Cloud Sync na-enweghị mbinye aka mgbe ahazighị nzuzo mpaghara (#13679). A na-enyocha mbinye aka ọ bụla dị adị mgbe niile — a na-ajụkwa ya mgbe niile ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` — n'agbanyeghị ọkọlọtọ a. Ndabara ga-agbanwe gaa na mmanye na v3.9.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ZED_IMPORT_LEGACY_ONE_STEP`      | `false`                                                   | `src/app/api/providers/zed/import/route.ts`      | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji laghachi na omume otu nzọụkwụ nke v3.8.5, "bubata ihe niile", na-enweghị nkwenye onye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                      | `http://localhost:20128`                                  | OAuth, Dashboard, mmekọrịta                      | URL ọha maka OAuth redirect_uri, njikọ Dashboard, na URL ọha ndị emepụtara. Tọọ nke a ka ọ bụrụ URL ọha kwụsiri ike mgbe OAuth callbacks ma ọ bụ njikọ ihe nchọgharị emepụtara ga-eji canonical reverse-proxy host.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL`                     | _(efu)_                                                   | Akụkụ onye ahịa                                  | Oyiri `CLOUD_URL` nke dị n'akụkụ onye ahịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`                       | _(edoghị ya)_                                             | `src/shared/services/cloudSyncScheduler.ts`      | Nhọrọ ndabere ochie maka `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_PORT`                          | _(edoghị ya — ọ na-alaghachi na `PORT`)_                  | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`          | Port ndabere nke dị n'akụkụ onye ahịa maka URL ngosi mgbe a maghị origin (SSR/ule); a na-agụ ya tupu `PORT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                 | _(edoghị)_                                                | Onye na-achọpụta public-origin, URL onyonyo      | Ebe mmalite OmniRoute nke ihe nchọgharị na-eji, nke nwere mkpa kachasị elu maka imepụta URL ọha na nkwado izi ezi nke browser-origin na-abụghị dashboard. Tọọ nke a mgbe OpenWebUI ma ọ bụ relay ọzọ na-eji URL ime ụlọ enweta OmniRoute, mana ihe nchọgharị onye ọrụ ga-ebudata mgbasa ozi emepụtara site na LAN, tunnel, ma ọ bụ ebe mmalite ọha. Etinyekwala `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL`           | _(edoghị)_                                                | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | URL zuru oke nke manifest plugin provider a na-akpọsa nye sidecar clients. Mgbe edoghị ya, OmniRoute na-ewepụta `/api/v1/provider-plugin-manifest` site na ebe arịrịọ si malite ma ọ bụ HOST/PORT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`                 | `http`                                                    | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | Protocol a na-eji mgbe a na-ewepụta URL manifest plugin provider site na HOST/PORT na-enweghị ebe arịrịọ si malite. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `https` n'azụ public proxy na-akwụsị TLS mgbe edoghị `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL` kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                     | _(edoghị)_                                                | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`              | Ụdị ntụkwasị obi nhọrọ maka headers public-origin e bufere. Mgbe edoghị ya = atụkwasịla `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` obi maka mkpebi nchekwa. `true` / `loopback` na-atụkwasị host/proto e bufere obi naanị site na loopback proxy nwere token. `private` / `lan` na-atụkwasịkwa ndị proxy nọ na private-LAN obi, na-ahapụ bridge `API_PORT` ka ọ debe `X-Forwarded-For` nke onye dị otú ahụ, ma na-ahapụ routes LOCAL_ONLY ịnakwere client nọ na private network nke proxy dị otú ahụ kọrọ (ọ dịghị mgbe ọ bụ arịrịọ Cloudflare-tunnel, chain loopback-only, ma ọ bụ loopback-only CLI-token gate). Họrọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` doro anya na production.                                                                                 |
| `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                          | _(edoghị)_                                                | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | URL callback ọha maka ọrụ kie.ai asynchronous. Override nwere mkpa kachasị elu tupu `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL` na `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                | _(edoghị)_                                                | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Ụzọ ọzọ e si ede `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`. A na-eji ya dị ka fallback mgbe edoghị variable mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`                      | _(edoghị)_                                                | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Ebe mmalite ọha a na-eji emepụta URL callback asynchronous. Fallback nwere mkpa kacha ala maka callback kie.ai; a na-ejikwa ya dị ka URL ọha izugbe maka relay ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CROF_USAGE_URL`                  | `https://crof.ai/usage_api/`                              | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint ọchụchọ oke ojiji CrofAI nke ibe Usage na-eji. Mee override maka relay / test fixture.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLINEPASS_USAGE_URL`             | `https://api.cline.bot/api/v1/users/me/plan/usage-limits` | `open-sse/services/usage/clinepass.ts`           | Ebe njedebe oke ojiji ClinePass nke awa 5 / kwa izu / kwa ọnwa nke ibe Ojiji na-eji. Gbanwee ya maka relays / nhazi ule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPENCODE_QUOTA_URL`              | `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1/usage`                     | `open-sse/services/opencodeQuotaFetcher.ts`      | Ebe njedebe ojiji OpenCode Go nke ukara, nke API key na-eme nkwenye njirimara ya, nke ibe Ojiji na-eji. Gbanwee ya maka relays / nhazi ule.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`           | `true`                                                    | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Mepụta headers njirimara OpenCode CLI (User-Agent, x-opencode-client/project, UUID arịrịọ/seshọn) n'arịrịọ upstream opencode-go/zen ndị client ahụ na-ezigaghị, ka Cloudflare dị na ụzọ ọpụpụ VPS nwee ike ịnakwere ha (#6210/#5997). Agbanyere ya na ndabara kemgbe #10571; jiri `false`/`0`/`no`/`off` gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                       | `opencode/1.18.31`                                        | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | User-Agent ndabara a na-eji mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị ọkụ ma enweghị mgbanwe `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` ahaziri maka provider ọ bụla. A na-etinye ya naanị na executors opencode. N'arịrịọ na-enweghị key nke upstream na-egbochi, a ga-eji ndabara a dochie uru ahaziri nke na-enweghị `opencode/<version >= 1.17>` kama ịjụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARD_ATTRIBUTION`             | `1` (agbanyeere)                                          | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | Ziga headers njirimara-ngwa client (`x-session-id`, `x-title`, `http-referer`) n'ihu nye providers upstream, ka arịrịọ malitere n'ime harness edepụtara wee jigide njirimara ya mgbe ọ na-agafe router (ọnụ ụzọ publisher OpenRouter na-adabere na ndị a). `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` na-edobe njirimara registry/operator ma na-ewepụ ụkpụrụ client nyere. Headers omenala operator haziri na-akarị ụkpụrụ client mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                           | `desktop`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka header `x-opencode-client` emepụtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị ọkụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                          | `global`                                                  | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka header `x-opencode-project` emepụtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị ọkụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_IP_LOCK_PROVIDERS`        | _(edoghị)_                                                | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`          | Nchịkọta providers dị irè nke a na-ekewa ọdịda upstream ha dịka IP ụzọ ọpụpụ si dị (otu 429 na-adọba njikọ nwanne niile na-eji otu IP ahụ). Mgbe edoghị ya, ọ na-eji ndabara ọkwa efu `opencode,opencode-go,opencode-cli`; `none`/`off`/`false`/`0` na-agbanyụ usoro ọdịda a kpamkpam; ndepụta ejiri rikoma kewaa na-anọchi nchịkọta ahụ. Atụmatụ akwụ ụgwọ ka upstream na-ekewa dịka akaụntụ si dị, ya mere ha nwere ike ịgbanyụ ya n'enweghị nsogbu (#9611).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_QUOTA_ROTATION_PROVIDERS` | _(edoghị)_                                                | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`          | Oke ntụgharị egress maka ọjụjụ quota. Mgbe edoghị ya, ọ na-ewepụ onye otu pool nwa oge n'ihi 429 naanị maka ezinụlọ e ji IP kewaa n'elu; `all` na-agbatị ndekọ ahụ ruo provider niile, nke mere na rate limit e nwetara site n'aka otu onye otu ga-eme ka nhọpụta na-esote jiri egress ọzọ. A na-eleghara uru ọ bụla na-abụghị `all` anya. Ọ chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED` (agbanyere ya na ndabara). A na-eji adreesị egress eme key nke store ahụ, ọ bụghị (provider, address), ya mere 429 agbasapụrụ agbasapụ na-adọba onye otu ahụ maka provider niile ruo mgbe oge ahụ gafere (nkeji 5, na-amụba okpukpu abụọ ruo 15). A na-agụ ya maka nsonaazụ nzaghachi ọ bụla, ya mere mgbanwe na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ na-enweghị ịmalitegharịa. |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_REQUEST_CONTRACT`       | _(edoghị)_                                                | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `off` iji kwụsị imegharị body nke arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị key (streaming flag na ndepụta tool). A ka na-etinye header ndị ahụ. A na-agụ ya maka arịrịọ ọ bụla, ya mere mgbanwe na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_PLACEHOLDER_TOOLS`      | _(edoghị)_                                                | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Aha tool ndị e ji rikoma kewaa nke a ga-ekwupụta n'arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị key nke na-enweghị tool ọ bụla, mgbe a hụbeghị ihe ọ bụla maka model ahụ. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo, ọ na-alaghachi na otu placeholder nke a gwara model ahụ ka ọ ghara ịkpọ. Ọ kachasị ndenye 32, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; a na-eleghara ndenye na-adịghị mma anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_CREDENTIAL`             | `public`                                                  | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Credential a na-eziga n'ụzọ free-tier e tinyere mgbochi (`https://opencode.ai/inference/v1`). Kemgbe 2026-10-21, ụzọ ahụ na-eji 403 `Model access is disabled` aza ezigbo API key ma na-enye literal anonymous ahụ kama. `key` na-edobe API key nke operator, `none`/`off` anaghị eziga header credential ọ bụla, a na-eziga literal ọ bụla ọzọ dịka bearer token. A na-agụ ya maka arịrịọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_URL`          | `https://ollama.com/settings`                             | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | URL ntọala Ollama Cloud a na-eji maka ịnakọta quota. Dochie ya maka relay / test fixture.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_BALANCE_URL`        | `https://ollama.com/api/balance`                          | `open-sse/services/ollamaCloudBalance.ts`        | Endpoint balance upstream Ollama Cloud edere n'akwụkwọ (`GET https://ollama.com/api/balance`, `Authorization: Bearer <API key>`). A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe module na-ebudata. A na-ebu ụzọ jiri ya maka quota Ollama Cloud mgbe connection nwere API key; scraper cookie nke ibe ntọala na-anọgide dịka fallback. Dochie ya maka relay / test fixture.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`                       | _(edoghị)_                                                | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Cookie Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` a na-eji maka ịnakọta quota site na ibe ntọala. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; mgbe ahaziri account dị iche iche, họrọ iji field Dashboard nke connection ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`                 | _(edoghị)_                                                | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Env var cookie Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` ọzọ. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; mgbe ahaziri account dị iche iche, họrọ iji field Dashboard nke connection ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`             | _(unset)_                                                 | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Env var ọzọ maka kuki Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session`, nke a na-eji tupu aha mkpirisi ndị ọzọ. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ, ọ ka mma iji oghere Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`          | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`           | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint oke ojiji CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). Gbanwee ya maka relays / test fixtures.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Mgbe ị na-ebunye n'azụ reverse proxy (nginx, Caddy), tọọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ URL ọha kwụụrụ onwe ya (dịka ọmụmaatụ, `https://omniroute.example.com`) mgbe callbacks OAuth ma ọ bụ njikọ ọha ndị emepụtara ga-eji hostname ahụ. Enweghị nke a, callbacks OAuth nwere ike ịda n'ihi na redirect_uri agaghị adakọ, njikọ ọha ndị emepụtara nwekwara ike iziga na origin ime container ahụ.
>
> Debe `BASE_URL` dịka URL loopback/container ime maka ọrụ server-to-server. Ejila `Origin` nke ihe nchọgharị ma ọ bụ hostname ọha maka internal self-fetches ndị na-ebu credentials.
>
> Ndekọ dashboard ndị e mere authentication anaghị achọ base URL ọha nke edobere otu ebe: dashboard na-eziga arịrịọ same-origin ndị nwere ike ịgbanwe data, tinyere token CSRF nke ejikọtara na session. OmniRoute ka na-achịkọta nkwado public-origin n'otu ebe maka njikọ ihe nchọgharị ndị na-abụghị nke dashboard: a na-ebu ụzọ tụkwasị env vars URL ọha ndị akọwapụtara obi; a na-eleghara headers `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` ndị na-agbanwebeghị anya, belụsọ ma agbanyere `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` ma jiri token kaa proxy peer kacha nso akara dịka nke a tụkwasịrị obi. Ejila ntọala CORS dozie arịrịọ dashboard same-origin; CORS bụ naanị maka clients ihe nchọgharị cross-origin.

---

## 8. Proxy Ọpụpụ

Dufe oku ndị na-enye LLM n'elu site na proxy HTTP ma ọ bụ SOCKS5 maka ịchịkwá ọpụpụ, ịkụzi ụzọ geo, ma ọ bụ izochi IP.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` na-abụ `true` dị ka ndabara. Tinye ya na `false`, `0`, `no`, ma ọ bụ `off` iji manye HTTP/1.1 na ndị nkesa Undici (dispatchers) dị na `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts` na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: ọpụpụ ozugbo, proxy HTTP(S)/SOCKS, ndị relay, na nnwalegharị socket ọhụrụ ha. Nke a na-echekwa nyocha asambodo TLS, nyocha njirimara proxy, ókè njikọ, na ntọala keep-alive. Malitegharịa OmniRoute mgbe ị gbanwere ya, n'ihi na a na-echekwa ndị nkesa ahụ ma a na-emepụta ndị nnọchi relay na mmalite. Ịgbanwe a anaghị achịkwa wreq TLS fingerprinting ma ọ bụ ụzọ mbufe pụrụ iche nke onye na-enye ọrụ.

| Agbanwe                                  | Ndabara           | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                              | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`            | `open-sse/executors`                           | Mee ka nọchiteanya SOCKS5 proxy rụọ ọrụ maka oku upstream. Jiri `false` wepụ onwe gị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`            | N'akụkụ onye ahịa                              | Ịmata n'akụkụ onye ahịa banyere nnweta SOCKS5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`            | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Okọlọtọ atụmatụ nke na-arụ ọrụ n'ụzọ ndabara (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); nkwusi dashboard DB ka emeri). Ngwakọta proxy na ntụgharị kwa-akaụntụ na-akwụsị ịnyegharị ọzọ onye otu nke dara ọdụ n'oge na-adịbeghị anya (nyocha TCP a jụrụ, ma ọ bụ 429 site na ya) ruo oge nke na-agba abụọ mgbe ọ megharịrị, ruo n'oke. `false` na-eweghachi nhọrọ nkịtị.                                                          |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(a na-edobeghị)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Ndepụta-ekwere maka ebumnuche nchịkwa-nhọrọ nke ime-ime gafere loopback (ndị ọbịa ma ọ bụ IP e kewapụrụ site na kọma/ọghere, `*` na-emeghe LAN/private ma ọ bụghị metadata/link-local mgbe ọzọ). A na-ekwe loopback site na http/https n'ụzọ ndabara. Uru dashboard DB na-emeri agbanwe a mgbe edobere ya ma ọ bụghị efu.                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Oge ntọala (ms) nke mwepụ 429, nke na-agba abụọ mgbe ọ megharịrị. A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe mmalite. A kechiri ya n'ime `1000..3600000`; nke gafere oke ma ọ bụ nke na-agụghị na-alaghachi na ndabara tinyere ịdọ aka ná ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Oke (ms) nke mwepụ 429. A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe mmalite. A kechiri ya n'ime `1000..3600000`; nke gafere oke ma ọ bụ nke na-agụghị na-alaghachi na ndabara tinyere ịdọ aka ná ntị. Mgbe oke dị n'okpuru ntọala, ọ na-alaghachi na ndabara. Ibelata oke ahụ na-ebelatakwa ichefu usoro: ndepụta na-akpochapụ `2 x max` mgbe oge gachara.                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`           | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Okọlọtọ atụmatụ nke chọrọ itinye aka (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); nkwusi dashboard DB ka emeri). Maka ndị na-enye nke a kenyere kesi n'ịdọba site na adreesị egress, hazie onye otu ngwakọta nke na-ekekọrịta adreesị egress a hụrụ nke onye otu a jụrụ n'oge na-adịbeghị anya n'okpuru ndị otu dị mma. Nhazi naanị, a naghị apụ ya. Ọ chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, nke na-emepụta mgbaàmà ọjụjụ ọ na-agụ. |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`          | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Windo mbịaru (ms) na-ejide ịdọ aka ná ntị webhook `proxy.set_aside` ugboro ugboro maka onye otu ngwakọta ọ bụla ka nnukwu ọjụjụ ziga otu mmemme. A na-agụ ya mgbe ọ na-emepụta ọ bụla. A kechiri ya n'ime `1000..3600000`; nke gafere oke ma ọ bụ nke na-agụghị na-alaghachi na ndabara na-enweghị mkpọtụ.                                                                                                                        |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(a na-edobeghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | HTTP proxy maka oku upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(a na-edobeghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | HTTPS proxy maka oku upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(a na-edobeghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Proxy zuru ụwa ọnụ (na-akwado `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(edobeghị)_      | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Na-edobe ebe lekwasị anya echo-IP nke nyocha egress proxy na-eji na otu URL. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, nyocha ahụ na-anwa `api64.ipify.org` tupu `api4.ipify.org` ka a ghara ịkọ tunnels IPv4 naanị dị ka ndị nwụrụ anwụ (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(edobeghị)_      | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | Aha host/IP kewapụrụ site na comma iji gafee proxy ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`              | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Ọnụ ọgụgụ sockets kacha elu n'otu oge maka onye nkesa proxy HTTP/SOCKS a kwadoro n'eche. Osimiri SSE dị ogologo dị ka Codex `/v1/responses` chọrọ karịa otu njikọ mgbe ọtụtụ arịrị na-ekekọrịta otu proxy n'ọkwa akaụntụ. A na-edobe oke ụkpụrụ karịa `256`.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`           | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | Oge njedebe handshake SOCKS5 (njikọ) na ms. Welite ya mgbe otu ebe obibi gateway host nwetara concurrency dị elu (dịka ọmụmaatụ arịrị 100 n'otu oge) — handshake n'ezie nwere ike ịgafere 10s n'okpuru pool juru n'afọ ọbụlagodi na a nwere ike iru proxy ahụ, nke ga-apụta na ọzọzọ dị ka `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable` adịgboroja. Edobere oke na `120000`.                                                             |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`           | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ndabara), a na-**jụ (fail-closed)** arịrị nke proxy e kenyere ya dara na ịdozi kama ịlaghachi na njikọ kpọmkwem — nke na-egbochi mwepụ IP n'ezie. Tọọ `true` iji weghachi usoro DIRECT fallback ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`           | `open-sse/executors`                           | Jiri wreq-js ghọọ TLS fingerprint (na-akọ Chrome 124). Na-eboju mgbochi JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(edobeghị)_      | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Ndepụta ndị na-enye ọrụ a nabatara kewapụrụ site na comma maka ụzọ TLS proxied ọhụrụ (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). TLS kpọmkwem na-edebe omume ochie ya mgbe edobeghị ya; naanị ndị na-enye ọrụ ndị a ka na-agafee ọzara fingerprint Chrome-124. A wepụrụ Groq (`api.groq.com`) ọbụlagodi mgbe edobeghị ndepụta a nabatara, n'ihi na Chrome JA3 na-akpali Cloudflare 1010 ebe undici n'ala na-eru API ahụ.                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`           | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Kwe ka ihe ọdịnaya ihe nchọgharị Claude Turnstile Playwright leghara njehie asambodo HTTPS anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### Ọnọdụ

| Ọnọdụ                                            | Nhazi                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 site na ọwa SSH**                       | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                           |
| **Proxy HTTP ụlọ ọrụ**                           | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com` |
| **Mgbochi fingerprint**                          | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — chọrọ `wreq-js` (tinyere n'ime ya)                                                        |
| **Egress a na-achịkwa / enweghị ohere kpọmkwem** | Hapụ `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (ndabara). Arịrị na-adaa kpamkpam mgbe proxy adịghị kama ịkwụsa IP site na njikọ kpọmkwem.   |
| **Ochie / dev — kwe ka njikọ kpọmkwem daa**      | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Na-eweghachi omume tupu ịsie ike: a na-eji njikọ kpọmkwem mgbe ịdozi proxy dara.                  |

> **Mara (NVIDIA validation bypass — #3226):** Endpoint nkwenye igodo API nke NVIDIA
> na-akwụsịtụ mgbe a gafere ya site na fetch proxy/TLS-patched zuru ụwa ọnụ (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` na-agafee proxy patch n'echiche
> maka otu oku nkwenye ahụ site na iji `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Nke a bụ nnwere onwe e dere akwụkwọ nke nwere oke — ọ **dịghị** metụta egress chat/ojiji.
> Nnwale `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts` na-edobe oke bypass ahụ.

---

## 9. Mmekọrịta Ngwá Ọrụ CLI

Ọ na-achịkwa otu OmniRoute si achọpụta ma malite CLI sidecars (Claude Code, Codex, wdg.).

| Mgbanwe                           | Ndiizuzu                             | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                               | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | `auto` = chọọ PATH nke sistemụ; `manual` = jiri nanị ụzọ e kwupụtara n'ụzọ doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(a tọghị)_                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ndepụta PATH ndị ọzọ maka ịchọpụta binary CLI (e ji colon kewapụ ha).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(a tọghị)_                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Dochie ndekọ ụlọ maka ịgụ nhazi CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Ọ ga-abụrịrị absolute ma dịkwa n'ime ụlọ nke usoro (process) — **ma ọ bụ**, n'ime container, ụzọ e jiri bind-mount (nke ahụ bụ otu `/host-home` si arụ ọrụ). Ihe ọ bụla ọzọ ga-adachie na ndekọ ụlọ mbụ.                                                                                     |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                               | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Kwe ka OmniRoute dee faịlụ nhazi CLI (mmelite token, data nnọkọ). Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji mee ka ide nhazi CLI ọ bụla daa ada na njehie "writes disabled" doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_CLAUDE_BIN`                  | `claude`                             | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ ahaziri n'ụzọ pụrụ iche gaa na binary Claude CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CLI_CODEX_BIN`                   | `codex`                              | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ ahaziri n'ụzọ pụrụ iche gaa na binary Codex CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_DROID_BIN`                   | `droid`                              | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ ahaziri n'ụzọ pụrụ iche gaa na binary Droid CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_OPENCLAW_BIN`                | `openclaw`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ ahaziri n'ụzọ pụrụ iche gaa na binary OpenClaw CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_CURSOR_BIN`                  | `agent`, mgbe ahụ `cursor`           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ ahaziri n'ụzọ pụrụ iche gaa na binary agent Cursor. Ọ bụrụ na enweghị ya, nchọpụta ga-anwa `agent` buru ibu wee dachie na `cursor`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_CLINE_BIN`                   | `cline`                              | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ ahaziri n'ụzọ pụrụ iche gaa na binary Cline CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_5DIVE_BIN`                   | `5dive`                              | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ ahaziri n'ụzọ pụrụ iche gaa na binary CLI 5dive.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_5DIVE_STATE_DIR`             | `/var/lib/5dive`                     | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ndekọ ọnọdụ sistemụ nke 5dive (profaịlụ nyocha nke root nwe); na-egosipụta ndiizuzu STATE_DIR nke 5dive n'onwe ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CLI_CONTINUE_BIN`                | `cn`                                 | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ ahaziri n'ụzọ pụrụ iche gaa na binary Continue CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CLI_QODER_BIN`                   | `qodercli`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ ahaziri n'ụzọ pụrụ iche gaa na binary Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_QWEN_BIN`                    | `qwen`                               | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ ahaziri n'ụzọ pụrụ iche gaa na binary Qwen Code CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

| `CLI_GOOSE_BIN`                   | `goose`                              | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                   | Ụzọ ahaziri n'ụzọ pụrụ iche gaa na binary Goose CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_WHYCODES_BIN`                | `whycodes`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntimeWhyCodes.ts`           | Ụzọ ahaziri iche maka faịlụ binarị WhyCodes CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Ihe ndepụta abụọ dị na catalog (`hermes` na `hermes-agent`) na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                   | `devin`                              | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`                     | Ụzọ ahaziri gaa na binary CLI nke Devin (v3.8.0). Executor Windsurf/Devin na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DEVIN_DESKTOP_VERSION`           | `3.6.27`                             | `open-sse/executors/devin-desktop.ts`                 | `ide_version` nke Devin Desktop. Ihe nnọchi ọ bụla ga-eji usoro `x.y.z`; uru ndị ezighi ezi ga-alaghachi na ndabara e nyochaara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `DEVIN_DESKTOP_EXTENSION_VERSION` | `1.48.2`                             | `open-sse/executors/devin-desktop.ts`                 | `extension_version` nke Codeium/language-server e jikọtara ọnụ, dị iche na `ide_version` nke Desktop. Ihe nnọchi ga-eji `x.y.z`; uru ndị ezighi ezi ga-eji ndabara e jikọtara.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CLI_DEVIN_AGENTIC_BIN`           | `devin`                              | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli-agentic.ts`             | Ihe nnọchi Devin CLI maka naanị agentic bridge. Executor ahụ na-anabata naanị upstream ACP stdio nke local.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `DEVIN_AGENTIC_HOME`              | _(achọrọ)_                           | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli-agentic.ts`             | Ụlọ home zuru oke nke e kewapụrụ maka subprocess Devin agentic; ụzọ bridge a nabatara bụ `/home/bridge` na ụzọ `.sandbox` nke task ahụ (na Windows, `C:\...\.sandbox\...`).                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `DEVIN_AGENTIC_ACP_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `120000`                             | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli-agentic.ts`             | Oge kacha ogologo nke otu turn Devin ACP tupu bridge ebibie child process ahụ ma weghachite timeout doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `DEVIN_BRIDGE_MODEL`              | `devin-cli-agentic/swe-1-7`          | `docker/devin-bridge/compose.yml`                     | Alias nke isi model Claude Code maka bridge e kewapụrụ. Harness dị ndụ ga-anọchi ihe atụ ahụ na model akaụntụ Devin nke ugbu a weghachitere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `DEVIN_BRIDGE_SONNET_MODEL`       | `DEVIN_BRIDGE_MODEL`                 | `docker/devin-bridge/compose.yml`                     | Alias nke bridge e kewapụrụ a na-eji mgbe Claude Code rịọrọ ndabara Sonnet ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `DEVIN_BRIDGE_OPUS_MODEL`         | `DEVIN_BRIDGE_MODEL`                 | `docker/devin-bridge/compose.yml`                     | Alias nke bridge e kewapụrụ a na-eji mgbe Claude Code rịọrọ ndabara Opus ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `DEVIN_BRIDGE_HAIKU_MODEL`        | `DEVIN_BRIDGE_MODEL`                 | `docker/devin-bridge/compose.yml`                     | Alias nke bridge e kewapụrụ a na-eji mgbe Claude Code rịọrọ ndabara Haiku ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `DEVIN_BRIDGE_SUBAGENT_MODEL`     | `DEVIN_BRIDGE_MODEL`                 | `docker/devin-bridge/compose.yml`                     | Alias nke bridge e kewapụrụ a na-eji maka subagents nke Claude Code.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `DEVIN_SEAT_API_URL`              | `https://server.codeium.com`         | `open-sse/services/usage/devinCli.ts`                 | Nnọchi anya nhọrọ maka API njikwa oche (seat-management) Codeium nke quota Devin CLI na-eji (`GetUserStatus`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `AUGGIE_BIN`                      | `auggie`                             | `open-sse/executors/auggie.ts`                        | Nnọchi anya nke ụzọ zuru oke (absolute path) maka binary CLI Augment (Auggie) nke provider `auggie` mpaghara na-eji. Ọ na-alaghachi na `CLI_AUGGIE_BIN`, mgbe ahụ ọ na-achọ na PATH.                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLI_AUGGIE_BIN`                  | `auggie`                             | `open-sse/executors/auggie.ts`                        | Nnọchi anya aha ọzọ (alias) maka ụzọ binary CLI Augment (Auggie) (a na-ele ya mgbe `AUGGIE_BIN` gasịrị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ZCODE_BIN`                       | `zcode`                              | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                         | Binary e ji arụ ọrụ maka stdio client nke provider `zcode` mpaghara. Ọ na-alaghachi na `zcode` dị na PATH.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ZCODE_ARGS`                      | —                                    | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                         | Nhazi JSON (≤16 strings) nke arguments ọzọ a na-enyefe binary `zcode` mgbe e malitere ya site na `cliTools`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ZCODE_CWD`                       | `process.cwd()`                      | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                         | Directory ọrụ maka subprocess nke ZCode app-server.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ZCODE_TRANSPORT`                 | `app-server`                         | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                         | `app-server` (ndabara) ma ọ bụ `gateway`. **Nke nnwale / anaghị akwado ya:** `gateway` na-eziga arịrịọ HTTP ozugbo na Z.AI Coding Plan site n'ịmegharị protocol nke client ZCode (handshake mbinye aka na header ihe akaebe ọrụ nke client 0.16.9), ọ nwekwara ike ịkwụsị ịrụ ọrụ mgbe ZCode emelitere. Ọ na-anabata naanị profaịlụ Individual Coding Plan. |
| `ZCODE_PROVIDER_ID`               | `account:zai-individual-coding-plan` | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                         | Profaịlụ onye na-enye ọrụ nke ala kpọmkwem maka ịhọrọ model. Mgbe a na-etinyeghị ya, a na-eji profaịlụ akaụntụ Individual Plan, na ụzọ ndabere `builtin:zai-coding-plan` (ntinye nhazi nke nwere igodo Individual Coding Plan). Tinye ID kpọmkwem dịka `account:zai-start-plan` iji jide profaịlụ ahụ; profaịlụ ejidere anaghị alaghachi n'ụzọ ndabere.     |
| `ZCODE_SERVER_RUNTIME_ROOT`       | `~/.zcode/server`                    | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                         | Mgbọrọgwụ nke runtime ZCode app-server (ebe `node` na `zcode-server.cjs` ejikọtara ọnụ bi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ZCODE_SERVER_NODE`               | `<runtimeRoot>/node`                 | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                         | Node executable e ji anabata ZCode app-server.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `ZCODE_SERVER_ENTRY`              | `<runtimeRoot>/zcode-server.cjs`     | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                         | Script ntinye app-server e ji anabata ZCode server.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ZCODE_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `10000`                              | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                         | Oge njedebe mmalite (ms) tupu a gụọ mmalite ZCode app-server dị ka nke dara ada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ZCODE_RPC_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `30000`                              | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                         | Oge njedebe RPC kwa arịrị (ms) maka oku ZCode app-server.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ZCODE_TURN_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `120000`                             | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                         | Ogologo oge kacha elu (ms) nke otu ZCode turn tupu onye nlekọta (supervisor) kwụsịtụ ya n'ihi oge agwụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ZCODE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`          | `250`                                | `open-sse/executors/zcode.ts`                         | Oge nlele ugboro ugboro (ms) maka ịmata ma ZCode turn rụchara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `HERMES_HOME`                     | `~/.hermes`                          | `src/lib/cli-helper/config-generator/hermesHome.ts`   | Directory ụlọ (home) nke Hermes Agent ebe OmniRoute na-agụ/edega config Hermes CLI. Ọ dabara na env var nke installer Hermes PowerShell na-edobe na Windows (`%LOCALAPPDATA%\hermes`).                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `WHYCODES_HOME`                   | `~/.whycodes`                        | `src/lib/cli-helper/config-generator/whycodesHome.ts` | Na-edochi mgbọrọgwụ WhyCodes nke nwere `config.toml`. Mgbe edoghị ya, ọ na-achọta `.whycodes` n'okpuru `HOME`, e mesịa `USERPROFILE`, e mesịa `os.homedir()`. A na-eji uru nnọchi ahụ kpọmkwem dịka e nyere ya; a naghị agbasawanye `~` dị na mbido.                                                                                                        |

### Nhazi-akpaaka CLI Profile (Auto-Sync)

Feature flags ndị a bụ nke opt-in, ma n'ụzọ ndabara ha adịghị arụ ọrụ. Ị nwekwara ike ịgbanwe ha site na CLI Code dashboard.

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara | Faili Isi Iyi                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe nhazi model provider gasịrị, ọ na-edekwa ọzọ na-akpaghị aka faili profile `~/.codex/*.config.toml` site na catalog dị ndụ. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ gaghị agbanwe config Codex na-arụ ọrụ/ndabara, auth, ntọala Codex-lb, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ provider.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe nhazi model provider gasịrị, ọ na-edekwa ọzọ na-akpaghị aka faili profile Claude Code `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` site na catalog dị ndụ. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ gaghị agbanwe config Claude na-arụ ọrụ/ndabara, auth, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ provider. |

### Ihe Atụ Docker

```bash
# Tinye binaries nke host n'ime container ma gwara OmniRoute ebe ha dị:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` na-arụ ọrụ naanị mgbe e bind-mounted ụzọ ahụ n'ezie site na host — jikọta ya na mounts dị ka `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (lee profaịlụ `host` na `docker-compose.yml`). A na-eleghara ụzọ ọ bụla nke adịghị n'ime ụlọ onye ọrụ container ma ọ bụ bụrụ bind mount anya, n'ihi na ihe e dere ebe ahụ ga-atụfu mgbe a mepụtara container ọzọ.

Image ahụ na-agba ọsọ dị ka `USER node`, n'ihi ya `/root` na-enweghị mount **abụghị** override ziri ezi.

| Varyabụl                                 | Ndabara        | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------------- | -------------- | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(akpaaka)_    | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Mee ka njirimara container rụọ ọrụ (`1`/`true`) ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ ya (`0`/`false`). Ọ dị mkpa naanị na runtimes nke njirimara akpaaka na-eleghara anya.                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`        | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Kwe ka a dee CLI-tool config n'ime ụzọ container na-enweghị mount na agbanyeghị. Ihe hàtara ya na CLI bụ `--allow-container-write`.                                                                                                                          |
| `DOCKER_UPDATE_TRIGGER_FILE`             | enweghị ntọala | `src/lib/system/autoUpdate.ts`      | Naanị Docker image: dashboard **Melite Ugbu a** na-ede arịrịọ JSON na faịlụ a (kwesịrị ịdị n'ime `DATA_DIR`) ka onye nleba anya n'akụkụ host wee pull ma mepụta container ọzọ. Lee [Ntuziaka Docker](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#dashboard-updates-in-docker). |

### Ndị enyemaka CLI Binary (`omniroute`)

Varyabụl ndị a na-ahazi omume nke `omniroute` CLI binary n'onwe ya (ọ bụghị njirimara sidecar dị n'elu).

| Varyabụl                              | Ndabara                           | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(sistemụ)_                       | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Mee asụsụ mmepụta CLI n'ike. BCP-47 locale (dịka `en`, `pt-BR`). Ọ na-akagbuo system locale env vars (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Tinye ya na `1` iji ziga server stdout/stderr na terminal na supervised mode. Ihe hàtara ya bụ `--log` flag na `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token machine-auth etinyere dị ka header `x-omniroute-cli-token`. A na-emepụta ya na-akpaaka na task 8.12.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | Gemini home root na-adịgide adị; ọrụ one-shot na-eji ụlọ nwa oge ekewapụrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`                            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Oge ebere na milliseconds tupu e ji ike gbuo CLI child a nyela signal.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Tinye na 1 site na DATA_DIR kpọmkwem iji gafee faịlụ environment nke data-directory onye ọrụ ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNC_TOKEN`                | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/cli/commands/sync.mjs`             | Sync token maka `omniroute sync bundle` (`GET /api/sync/bundle` na-anabata naanị sync token, ọ bụghị API key). Ihe hàtara ya bụ `--token`.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`                           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | HTTP timeout kwa mgbalị (ms) maka arịrịọ CLI → server.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Oge kacha ogologo (ms) CLI na-echere server health endpoint tupu ọ pịnye ịdọ aka ná ntị banyere timeout. Ọ bara uru maka cold starts na-adị nwayọ (dịka Windows). Enwere ike ịtọkwa ya site na `--ready-timeout`.                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`                               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Tinye na `1` iji pịnye nyocha retry/backoff na stderr n'oge iwu CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Directory ahaziri maka ịchọpụta CLI plugin (`omniroute-cmd-*` packages). Ọ na-akwusi na `~/.omniroute/plugins/` mgbe a na-esetịpụghị ya. Naanị CLI — ọ naghị eru server-side plugin scanner, nke `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` na-ezipụta (ngalaba 2).                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`        | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Na-agbanyụ nyocha mmelite mgbe etinyere uru ọ bụla nke na-abụghị efu: ọkwa CLI mgbe usoro ọrụ kwụsịrị na banner “mmelite dị” na dashboard (`GET /api/system/version` na-amafe ọchụchọ npm, GitHub na akụkọ mgbe ahụ). Ọkwa CLI na-anọgide agbanyụrụ na CI, ọnọdụ dị jụụ na mmepụta nke na-abụghị ederede.          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCTOR_LIVENESS_URL`       | _(ewepụtara)_                     | `bin/cli/commands/doctor.mjs`           | Liveness URL nke `omniroute doctor` na-enyocha. Nhọrọ `livenessUrl` nke mmemme na-edu ụzọ; ọ bụghị ya, a na-eji uru a dị ka ọ dị. Mgbe a na-esetịpụghị ya, CLI na-ewepụta endpoint `/api/health/degradation` nke dashboard mpaghara.                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_CATALOG_PATH`     | `src/shared/constants/providers/` | `bin/cli/provider-catalog.mjs`          | Ụzọ provider catalog nke iwu CLI provider na-eji. Nhọrọ `catalogPath` nke mmemme na-edu ụzọ. A na-eji ụzọ zuru oke (absolute) dị ka ha dị; ụzọ metụtara (relative) na-akpebi site na project root. Mgbe a na-esetịpụghị ya, CLI na-eji provider constants directory, na faịlụ ochie `providers.ts` dị ka fallback. |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_TEST_MODEL`       | _(ndabara provider)_              | `bin/cli/provider-test.mjs`             | Model fallback zuru ụwa maka ule njikọ CLI provider. Usoro ọganihu bụ model njikọ ahaziri, model override kpọmkwem nye provider, uru a, mgbe ahụ model ule arụnyere n'ime provider.                                                                                                                                |
