## 10. Agent Ime Ụlọ & Mmekọrịta MCP

| Variable                                        | Default                                             | Source File                                                 | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | auto-detect                                         | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL e kwupụtara nke ngwá ọrụ MCP/A2A ga-eji rụe OmniRoute. Ọ na-ekpochapụ nchọpụta localhost akpaghị aka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(anahị edobe)_                                     | modul MCP/A2A                                               | Igodo API maka oku ngwá ọrụ MCP ime ụlọ na oku nkà A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(anahị edobe)_                                     | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | ID igodo maka nkenye ndekọ nyocha MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(anahị edobe)_                                     | Ochie                                                       | Aha ọzọ ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Ọnụ ọgụgụ ụbọchị akụkọ ọrụ A2A ka echekwara n'ọdụ data mpaghara tupu nhicha kwa ụbọchị ehichapụ ahịrị. Ọ bụrụ na anahị edobe ya, ọ bụghị ọnụ ọgụgụ, ma ọ bụ `<= 0`, ọ na-alaghachi na `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Kill-switch maka atụmatụ nleba anya nke memory-hits A2A. Tọọ ya na `0` iji gafee nchọpụta nlọghachi ncheta maka otu ọrụ kpamkpam; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (gụnyere ịghị edobe ya) na-eme ka ọ nọgide na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Na-arụ ọrụ njedebe recorded-triage nke Issue Agent offline/mpaghara. Hapụ ka ọ ghara ịrụ ọrụ belụsọ na ị na-agba ọsọ usoro ọrụ recorded-triage mpaghara n'ụzọ doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(anahị edobe)_                                     | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Oge njedebe (ms) maka otu ọsọ recorded-triage nke Issue Agent. E jigidere ya na oke kacha elu ime ụlọ; ọ na-alaghachi na ọdabara e wuru n'ime mgbe anahị edobe ya ma ọ bụ mgbe ọ bụghị nke bara uru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(ọnọdụ na-arụ ọrụ)_                                | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Ọnọdụ/profaịlụ CLI remote-mode maka iwu `omniroute`; ọ na-ekpochapụ ọrụ ọrụ na-arụ ọrụ n'ụlọ echekwara contexts mpaghara. Ọ bụ ihe yiri `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Na-agbanyụ backend OS-keychain `keytar` nhọrọ maka nzere ọnọdụ CLI. Mgbe a rụrụ ya ọrụ, nzere na-anọgide na `config.json` mode `0600` ma CLI na-ewepụta ịdọ aka ná ntị fallback otu ugboro naanị; e buro ya maka ọrụ headless/container e bu n'uche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Na-eme ka njikwa ohere dabere na scope rụọ ọrụ na oku ngwá ọrụ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

### Njikọ OAuth CLI (N'ime)

| Ọgbanwe             | Ihe ndabere             | Faịlụ isi mmalite               | Nkọwa                                             |
| ------------------- | ----------------------- | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | nchọpụta na-akpaghị aka | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL sava maka njikọ nyocha CLI↔OmniRoute.         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(a tọghị)_             | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Token nyocha maka njikọ CLI.                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`                   | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | ID onye ọrụ maka nnọkọ njikọ CLI.                 |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(a tọghị)_             | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ochie (legacy alias) maka `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`. |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(a tọghị)_             | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.                 |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(a tọghị)_             | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.               |

---

## 11. Ihe Nnweta Ndị Na-enye OAuth

### Mmelite token Codex n'ọnọdụ nnabata naanị

`OMNIROUTE_TOKEN_REFRESH_PASSIVE_PROVIDERS` etinyere na `codex` na-eme ka instance a bụrụ onye na-eji nzere Codex e mekọrịtara, ọ bụghị onye na-ahụ maka ịgbanwe refresh-token. Ndabara tọgbọ chakoo ma ọ bụ nke a na-etinyeghị na-edebe omume mmelite nkịtị. A na-ewepụ oghere dị n'akụkụ uru e kewara site na kọma, a naghị ekewakwa mkpụrụedemede ukwu na nta; `cx` bụ aha ọzọ maka `codex`. Ndị na-enye ọrụ a na-akwadoghị na-eme ka nkwenye mmalite daa. Ndị ọzọ na-enye ọrụ anaghị agbanwe.

N'ebe mmelite dị ugbu a, instance na-agafe cache nke ịgụ njikọ ma nabata naanị token ohere dị iche nke na-agafebeghị oge. Token upstream jụrụ n'oge arịrịọ enweghị ike ịghọ ndị a nabatara ọzọ mgbe ime ka data kwekọọ laghachiri azụ: nzere arịrịọ na-echekwa akara SHA-256 na-adịghị karịa anọ, a na-atụfu ha na arịrịọ ahụ. A na-anabata naanị mpaghara token, ya mere metadata onye na-enye ọrụ dị n'ime arịrịọ ka dị. A naghị echekwa nzere a nabatara na-adịgide adịgide. Ọ bụrụ na enweghị ihe ga-anọchi ya, `refresh_disabled` abụghị ọnọdụ ikpeazụ, a naghị agụkwa ya na circuit breaker nke mmelite token. Azịza upstream 401/403 mere n'ezie na-edebe ịgụ ọdịda nkịtị, oge ichere na-eto n'ụdị exponential na njehie a na-ezigara client; akara nkwụsị akaụntụ ka na-akpata mkpọchi na-adịgide adịgide.

Mmelite aka site na dashboard/API na-enye `409` nwere code `refresh_disabled` na ntụziaka ka emelite na instance nọ n'ọrụ ma mee ka data kwekọọ. Mmelite na-eme n'enweghị arịrịọ doro anya (chat, ojiji, nyocha ahụike na nkwenye mbubata) anaghị egosi njehie ahụ; mgbake 401 n'ịjụ ojiji mgbe achọrọ na-anwale ọzọ otu ugboro site na token e mekọrịtara a nabatara. Mbubata nkwenye Codex na-echekwa token e nyere na-enweghị mgbanwe site na refresh-token; nbanye OAuth ọhụrụ anaghị agbanwe. A naghị etinye ime ka data kwekọọ tupu iziga n'èzí ụzọ mmelite tupu oge dị ugbu a.

Debe naanị otu onye na-ahụ maka ịgbanwe token, haziekwa ime ka token kwekọọ iche, tupu token ohere agafe oge (oge mbụ ndabara maka mmelite: nkeji ise). Nhọrọ a abụghị mkpọchi kesara, mmegharị oyiri ebe nchekwa data ma ọ bụ ntinye SQLite a na-ekekọrịta. Ebe o si: `open-sse/services/tokenRefresh/passiveCodex.ts`.

Ihe nnweta e wuru n'ime maka **mmepe localhost**. Maka ndị arụnyere n'ebe dị anya, debanye nke gị na console onye mmepe nke onye na-enye ọ bụla.

Nke dabara: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

Ndabara `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_MAX_SESSIONS_PER_PROCESS`    | Qoder                   | Ngwaga ACP: megharịa usoro qodercli na-adịgide adịgide mgbe ọtụtụ nnọkọ dị otu a gachara. Ndabara `500`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

Mgbe URL ahụ bụ `/v1` nke OmniRoute n'onwe ya, sub-request describe na-ezipu `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` ma jiri nzere self-loop e kpebiri nyocha onwe ya (`sk_omniroute` sentinel na ọnọdụ mpaghara, ma ọ bụ `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) ka ntinye `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` wee rụọ ọrụ. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Nchekwa Vision Bridge   | Igodo API maka URL dị n'elu. Ọ na-ekpochapụ OpenAI / Google env vars kwa-onye na-ebubara maka oku vision-bridge na-abụghị nke Anthropic. Ụdị Anthropic na-edebe ụzọ igodo Anthropic pụrụ iche ha. Nsogbu #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Nchekwa Vision Bridge   | Ogologo oge a na-echekwa nsonaazụ nhazi "enweghị onye nhọrọ a ga-eji", na ms (ndabara `30000`). Ọnụọgụ ndị adịghị irè ma ọ bụ ndị ọjọọ na-adabereghachi na ndabara; `0` na-egbanyụọ cache ọjọọ ahụ. Isi: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Gaa na [Google Cloud Console → Credentials](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Mepụta OAuth 2.0 Client ID (ụdị: "Web application")
> 3. Tinye URL nkesa gị dị ka Authorized redirect URI
> 4. Dochie ọnụọgụ nzere ndị ahụ na `.env`.

## 12. Nkwakọ User-Agent nke Onye Na-enye

Kwakọ `User-Agent` header a na-eziga onye na-enye upstream ọ bụla. Executor base class na-edozi nke a n'ụzọ dynamic n'oge ọrụ (runtime):

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Mgbanwe | Uru ndabere | Oge ị ga-emelite ya |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.280 (external, cli)` | Mgbe Anthropic wepụtara ụdị CLI ọhụrụ |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true` iji zipu aha ngwá ọrụ nke third-party harness ozugbo (verbatim) ka ọ gaa na Anthropic n'ụzọ abụọ ahụ na-eduga n'Anthropic (native OAuth na CLIProxyAPI). N'ọnọdụ ndabere, executor na-enye aha ngwá ọrụ ndị ọzọ abụghị nke Claude Code aha ọzọ (alias) n'ụzọ a na-achọpụta — na-eji Claude Code canonical mapping ebe ọ dị, ma ọ bụghị PascalCase — ma tụgharịa ha n'azịza ahụ site na `_toolNameMap`, ka harness ndị nwere ngwá ọrụ snake_case ghara ịjụ dị ka ndị ahịa ndị ọzọ a chọpụtara site na fingerprint. Maka idozi mmejọ naanị. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Mgbe OpenAI melitere Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Kwakọ ụdị client Codex n'otu nke ya, n'enweghị isi na UA string zuru oke |
| `GROK_CLI_CLIENT_VERSION` | `1.0.41` | Kwakọ `x-grok-client-version` maka inference, mmekọ ụdị (model sync), na device-code OAuth, tinyere Grok CLI User-Agent ndabere maka inference na model sync. Ọ na-anabata SemVer nwere metadata prerelease/build nke ọ bụ nhọrọ, ruru mkpụrụedemede 128 mgbe a wépụrụ oghere (whitespace) gbara ya gburugburu. Ọnụ ọgụgụ ndị a na-atọghị, ndị efu, ndị ezighi ezi, ma ọ bụ ndị nwere line-break na-eji pin e zitere. Nkwakọ User-Agent inference zuru oke dị adị ka o na-ewere ọchịchị mbụ (precedence). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.280` | Kwakọ ụdị Claude Code e kwupụtara n'otu nke ya, n'enweghị isi na `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic na-akpachi ụfọdụ ụdị (models) n'ọnụ ọgụgụ a (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Kwakọ mgbakwụnye mkpụrụedemede atọ (3-character suffix) OmniRoute na-agbakwụnye na `cc_version=` n'ime Claude billing block. Bulie ya n'otu oge na `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` — ịgbatị naanị ụdị ahụ na-ekwupụta otu `version.revision` nke ọ bụghị ezigbo binary na-ezipụta. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.91` | Kwakọ ụdị Copilot CLI e kwupụtara n'otu nke ya, n'enweghị isi na `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Mgbe GitHub Copilot Chat melitere |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CLI_CL` | _(etinyeghị)_ | Na-anọchi ndepụta mgbanwe Piper nke Antigravity CLI (`agy`) a na-akọ dị ka `cl=` na User-Agent CLI. A pụghị isi na ụdị nweta ya, ọ dịghịkwa na manifests ọha; a na-ejuputa ya maka ụdị ọ bụla site na ndekọ e jidere n'ezie (1.4.4 → `1003595037`). Ọ bụrụ na etinyeghị ya maka ụdị a na-amaghị, a na-ahapụ `cl=` kama ịkpọsa ndepụta mgbanwe build ọzọ. |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Mgbe Kiro IDE melitere |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Kwakọ `clientId` nke Kiro social device-code (id ọha) |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Nhọrọ ịbanye (opt-in): nkwenye CRC zuru oke nke ozi ọ bụla n'ọkwa frame n'Kiro event stream (maka ịchọpụta ọwa ndị mebiri emebi) |
| `KIRO_CREDIT_PRICE_USD` | `0.02` | USD maka kredit Kiro ọ bụla; ọ na-agbanwe kredit `meteringEvent` ka ọ bụrụ ọnụ ahịa ziri ezi nke arịrị ọ bụla |
| `KIRO_REQUIRE_CREDITS` | `false` | Mee ka azịza Kiro na-ekwupụta na ọ nweghị nhazi kredit (credit metering) daa ụda, kama ịdekọ ya ka ọ bụ nke a na-adaghị ọnụ (unbilled) |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Mgbe Qoder CLI melitere |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Mgbe Cursor melitere |

> [!TIP]
> Ị nwere ike ịgbakwụnye nkwakọ User-Agent maka **ọ bụla** onye na-enye site na iji ụkpụrụ `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Executor na-ewulite aha mgbanwe gburugburu (env var) n'ụzọ dynamic.

## 13. Ndakọrịta Akara-Mkpịsịaka CLI

Mgbe agbanyere ya, OmniRoute na-ahazigharị isiokwu HTTP na mpaghara ahụ JSON ka ha kwekọọ kpọmkwem na akara njirimara nke ngwa CLI gọọmentị. Nke a na-ebelata ihe ize ndụ nke ịka akaụntụ gị akara ma na-echekwa IP proxy gị.

**Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Maka Onye Na-enye Ọrụ Ọ Bụla

| Mgbanwe                  | Mgbalite | Mmetụta                              |
| ------------------------ | -------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Codex CLI      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Claude Code    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ GitHub Copilot |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Antigravity    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Cursor         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Kimi Coding    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Kilo Code      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Cline          |

### Nke Izugbe

| Mgbanwe          | Mgbalite | Mmetụta                                                                 |
| ---------------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`     | Gbaa ndakọrịta akara-mkpịsịaka maka ndị na-enye ọrụ **niile** otu mgbe. |

### Mgbanwe njirimara Kimi Coding CLI

| Mgbanwe                 | Nke ndabara          | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                        | Nkọwa                                                            |
| ----------------------- | -------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`             | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Dochie ụdị Kimi CLI a na-eziga n'oge oku OAuth/API.              |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(ndabara e jidere)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Dochie ID ngwaọrụ Kimi e jidere nke a na-eji n'isiokwu klayenti. |

> [!NOTE]
> Njirimara a na-arụkọ ọrụ na mgbanwe User-Agent (§12). Sistemụ akara-mkpịsịaka na-ahụ maka nhazi isiokwu na nhazi mpaghara ahụ, ebe mgbanwe User-Agent na-ahụ maka eriri UA akọwapụtara. Enwere ike ịgbanye ha abụọ n'adabereghị onwe ha.

---

## 14. Ndị Na-enye Ọrụ Igodo API

Igodo API maka ndị na-enye ọrụ na-eji nyocha njirimara ozugbo. **Nhazi akwadoro:** Dashboard → Providers → Add API Key.

Ịtọ ha site na mgbanwe gburugburu bụ ụzọ ọzọ maka mbugharị Docker ma ọ bụ mbugharị na-enweghị ihu njikwa.

Ụkpụrụ a na-amata: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Mgbanwe            | Onye na-enye ọrụ                                     |
| ------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                             |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                           |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + Reader ndabere)            |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (aha ọzọ maka `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)             |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) embeddings + ndabere nkata |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (aha ọzọ maka `GEMINI_API_KEY`)               |

> [!NOTE]
> E wepụrụ ndenye `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` kwụ ọtọ maka Groq, xAI, Mistral, Perplexity, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, na Qianfan na v3.8.0 n'ihi na runtime anaghịzi agụ ha — ndị na-enye ọrụ ahụ na-adabere naanị na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB ezoro ezo. Hụ ngalaba _Nnyocha: Mgbanwe E Wepụrụ / Ndị Na-adịghị Arụ Ọrụ_ dị na njedebe akwụkwọ a maka ụzọ mbugharị.

> [!TIP]
> A na-echekwa igodo ndị edobere site na Dashboard n'ụdị ezoro ezo na SQLite, ha na-ebutekwa mgbanwe gburugburu ụzọ.
>
> **Jina:** embeddings `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, na `jina-search` anaghị akwụ ụgwọ igodo env klọsta mgbe njikọ dashboard `jina-ai` (ma ọ bụ `jina-reader` a na-ekekọrịta) dị — `getProviderCredentials` na-ebu ụzọ mejupụta. A na-eji `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard a pụrụ iji. Ndekọ oku na-egosi ndabere env dịka `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Kaadị Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) anaghị enye `/v1/embeddings` ma ọ bụ `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (aha ọzọ `google/gemini-embedding-2`) na-ebu ụzọ jiri njikọ dashboard `gemini`. A na-eji `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard a pụrụ iji. Ndekọ oku na-egosi ndabere env dịka `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Okporo data multimodal nke izizi na-eji `x-goog-api-key` megide `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — ihe N OpenAI `input` na-aghọ vector N.

---

## 15. Ntọala Oge-Ichere

Ụkpụrụ niile dị na **millisecond**. A na-achịkọta mkpebi niile n’ime `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Usoro Oge-Ichere

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (nnọchi izugbe)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (oku ndị na-enye ọrụ upstream, ndabara: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (na-enweta site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (na-enweta site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (na-enweta site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (onwe ya, ndabara: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (onwe ya, ndabara: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (onwe ya, ndabara: 0 = gbanyụrụ, a tụrụ aro: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (onwe ya, ndabara: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (onwe ya, ndabara: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (onwe ya, ndabara: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (na-enweta site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (nọọrọ onwe ya; edoghị ya: 1260000, oge ọrụ ndị ekwupụtara na-ebuli ya; edobere ya: oke siri ike; 0 na-agbanyụ ya)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (na-enweta site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (na-akpụ oke mgbatị njikere adapte, ndabara: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (na-enweta site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (ewepụtara, ndabara: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 0 = gbanyụrụ)
```

| Agbanwe                                         | Ndabara                                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(edoghị ahazi)_                                                  | Ụzọ mkpirisi zuru ụwa ọnụ — na-emeri ntọala ndabara nke `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` na `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Oge nkwụsị zuru ezu maka arịrịọ HTTP ndị a na-ezigara ndị na-enye ọrụ upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Oge kachasị nke ịgbachi nkịtị n’etiti byte raw upstream tupu a kwụsị arịrịọ ahụ. Model nwere echiche gbatịrị agbatị anaghị akwụsịkarị karịa sekọnd 90.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Oge kachasị nke ndụ stream SSE na-arụ ọrụ; ọ naghị amaliteghachi mgbe byte upstream bịara, ọ nọkwa onwe ya pụọ na `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. Mgbe edoghị ya ahazi, ndabara bụ `timeoutMs` kachasị n’ime model ọ bụla dị na ndekọ (1200000, Codex) tinyere oke sekọnd 60000, ma target nke kwupụtara oke oge dị ogologo karịa (njikọ `timeoutMs`, mgbe ahụ nke model, mgbe ahụ nke onye na-enye ọrụ, dịka GLM's 3000000) na-enweta oke oge ahụ tinyere sekọnd 60000. Uru dị mma bụ oke siri ike maka target ọ bụla nke oke oge ndị a kwupụtara enweghị ike ịbawanye. Tọọ ya na `0` iji gbanyụọ ya maka target niile.                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(agbanyụrụ)_                                                     | Ma OmniRoute nwere ike izipu ahịrị nkọwa SSE `:` (dịka `: keepalive` heartbeat na trailers metadata `x-omniroute-*`). A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara (#10524) n’ihi na ndị ahịa dakọtara na OpenAI siri ike na-eme JSON.parse n’ahịrị SSE ọ bụla ma daa mgbe ha hụrụ nkọwa `:`; heartbeat `data:` anaghị emetụta. Tọọ ya na `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` iji sonye ọzọ. `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `SSE_COALESCE_MS`                               | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                                                   | Nhọrọ ịgbanye: jikọta ihe omume Claude `content_block_delta` ndị na-esochi (otu block index na ụdị delta) a na-ezigara ndị ahịa Claude, na-ezipụ ha opekata mpe kwa N ms. Ọ naghị efu data; `signature_delta` na ihe omume oke anaghị ejikọta. Ọ na-emetụta ụdị delta niile ma ọ bụrụ na agbanwebeghị ya site na mgbanwe nke ụdị ọ bụla dị n'okpuru. `open-sse/utils/sseDeltaCoalesce.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SSE_COALESCE_THINKING_MS`                      | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                                                   | Mgbanwe nke ụdị kpọmkwem maka `SSE_COALESCE_MS` nke metụtara `thinking_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SSE_COALESCE_TEXT_MS`                          | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                                                   | Mgbanwe nke ụdị kpọmkwem maka `SSE_COALESCE_MS` nke metụtara `text_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SSE_COALESCE_INPUT_JSON_MS`                    | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                                                   | Mgbanwe nke ụdị kpọmkwem maka `SSE_COALESCE_MS` nke metụtara `input_json_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SSE_FRAME_COALESCE_MS`                         | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                                                   | Nhọrọ ịgbanye: chịkọta SSE frames ndị e bufere n'ime N ms ka ọ bụrụ otu idegara onye ahịa. A naghị egbu oge maka frame mbụ na frame mmechi (`[DONE]`, `message_stop`, `error`, `response.completed`/`failed`/`incomplete`); mgbe batching dị agbanwuru, ITL na-atụ oge dị n’etiti batch. `open-sse/utils/sseOutputSink.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SSE_STALL_WATCHDOG`                            | `timer`                                                           | Mmejuputa onye nche byte-stall maka nzaghachi ndị a na-asọba. `timer` na-amaliteghachi otu timer maka chunk ọ bụla si n'elu iyi (ọ na-arụ ọrụ kpọmkwem mgbe stall budget ruru); `interval` na-edobe otu nyocha maka stream ọ bụla, na oge `clamp(budget/4, 1s, 5s)`, ya mere stall na-arụ ọrụ n'ime budget + oge ahụ. `open-sse/utils/streamStallWatchdog.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Oge a na-ewe iji nata ihe omume SSE mbụ na-abụghị ping. Ọ na-eketa `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Oge kachasị nke windo njikere maka ihe omume mbụ na-agbanwe agbanwe maka arịrịọ nkwanye buru ibu, nke nwere ọtụtụ ngwaọrụ, ma ọ bụ nke chọrọ echiche miri emi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Mgba ọkụ nkwụsị maka heuristik `/goal`. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji kwụsị nchọpụta kpamkpam — a naghị eme ka oge njikere na mgbake nkwanye dị elu site na ahụ/isi arịrịọ, nke na-egbochi mmụba oge njedebe onye ahịa na-achịkwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Oge kacha elu nke windo njikere maka ihe omume mbụ maka ọsọ agent `/goal` a chọpụtara ma ọ bụ arịrịọ ndị a manyere site na `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Kwado mgbake nkwanye n’oge mbụ na-akpaghị aka maka ọsọ agent `/goal` a chọpụtara. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji kwụsị nhọrọ nnabata a metụtara goal. Nke a nwere ike naanị ịgbakwunye mgbake n’elu ndabara onye ọrụ — ọ naghị akagbu nhọrọ ịjụ nke `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/DB settings kwuru n’ụzọ doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Wepụ ihe omume SSE `codex.*` na-abụghị ọkọlọtọ (dịka `codex.rate_limits`) ndị na-eme ka `responses.stream()` nke OpenAI SDK daa na 502. Ndabara bụ ON (#11014). Tọọ `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` iji zipụ ha n’ihu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(unset)_                                                         | Ụgbọ njem Codex app-server a họọrọ n’ụzọ pụrụ iche. Ọgwụgwụ WebSocket (`ws://`/`wss://`) nke sidecar `codex app-server` dị na mpaghara. Mgbe edobere ya yana token, a na-ebugharị arịrịọ Codex site na JSON-RPC gaa na sidecar kama API HTTP Responses. A pụkwara ịtọ ya kwa njikọ site na `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(unset)_                                                         | Token ikike/bearer etinyere n’ahịrị a na-enye app-server. Ndochi kwa njikọ: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(unset)_                                                         | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ nwere token ikike app-server (site na `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). A na-eji ya mgbe `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN` na-adịghị setịpụ. Ndochi kwa njikọ: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Akwụkwọ ndekọ ọrụ ebe ntụgharị app-server na-arụ ọrụ. Ndochi kwa njikọ: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(unset)_                                                         | Usoro nkwado a na-enyefe ntụgharị app-server (dịka `never`, `on-request`). Ndochi kwa njikọ: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(unset)_                                                         | Usoro sandbox a na-enyefe ntụgharị app-server (dịka `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Mgbe edoghị ya, executor na-eji `workspace-write` na ndabara (nke e wusiri ike; na mbụ ọ bụ `danger-full-access`). Ndochi kwa njikọ: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Kwado onwe ya arịrịọ nkwado nke app-server n'onwe ya (ime iwu/faịlụ/ikikere n'isi igwe). A naghị agbanyere ya na ndabara — a na-ajụ arịrịọ ndị ahụ na-akpaghị aka; oku ngwaọrụ harness adịghị emetụta (ha na-agafe n'ụzọ `item/tool/call` dị iche). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`yes`. Ndochi kwa njikọ: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge a na-ewe iji nata isi nzaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_FETCH_START_TIMEOUT_CAP_MS`          | `110000`                                                          | Oge kacha a na-eche maka isi nzaghachi n'arịrịọ ndị na-asọ data (ms). `0` na-ewepụ oke a; `fetchStartTimeoutCapMs` nke onye na-enye ọrụ họpụtara nke ọma na-ebute ụzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Oge kacha echere mmalite nzaghachi (ms) maka mbọ ọ bụla kpọmkwem na-enweghị proxy. Oge gafere na-eme ka a nwalee ọzọ otu ugboro site na socket ọhụrụ; tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ oke a ma debe omume gara aga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`     | `300000` (5 min)                                                  | Oke kacha nta nke mmalite nzaghachi (ms) maka mbọ kpọmkwem na-enweghị proxy nke host ebumnuche ya bụ nkeonwe/LAN/loopback (dịka `localhost`, `host.docker.internal`, `192.168.x.x`), ka mmalite oyi nke Ollama dị n’ime ngwaọrụ ghara ịkwụsị site na ndabara 30s dị n’elu. Ọ na-arụ ọrụ dịka oke kacha nta: oge echere dị irè bụ nke ka ukwuu n’etiti uru a na `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-adịghị mma na-ewepụ oke kacha nta nke mpaghara. A naghị emetụta host ndị dịpụrụ adịpụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_VOLUME_ADAPTIVE`      | `1`                                                               | Welie windo mmalite nzaghachi ozugbo site na arịrịọ ịkwụ ụgwọ ụgwọ: ozu karịrị 512 KB ga-enweta × 2, karịa 2 MB × 3, kpuchie na 180 s na ọ dịghị ewetu ala dị elu dị elu (onye ọrụ na-agafe ma ọ bụ ala local/LAN n'elu). Nnukwu ọnọdụ na-ejupụta nke ọma ka ntinye nha na-enweghị onye na-ahọrọ echiche maka ụlọ echiche ka isi na ya, ya mere mpio dị larịị na-egbutu nnukwu mmiri na-agbago ngwa ngwa n'elu ala kpọmkwem. Tọọ `0`/`false`/`off` ka ọ tụnye omume dị larịị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (10 min)                                                 | Oke kachasị (ms) maka mbọ RETRY nke socket ọhụrụ mgbe oge mmalite nzaghachi nke mbọ pooled dị n’elu gafere (#13703). Ọ na-emetụta naanị mgbe onye na-akpọ oku tinyere akara njedebe nke ya (cascade nke njikọ/model/provider/`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` a kpebisiri ike); akara ahụ bụ oke kacha dị adị ma ọ na-ebute ụzọ n’ụzọ e zubere, ya mere nke a bụ nchekwa azụ sara mbara kama ịbụ okpu dị larịị — na-enweghị ya, retry ahụ na-eji otu windo `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` dị mkpirikpi nke mbọ pooled ahụ ma na-alaghachi 504 maka model echiche nwere TTFB dị ogologo ma dị mma. A naghị ekwe ka ọ dị ala karịa oke kacha nta dị larịị dị n’elu; mgbe onye na-akpọ oku enyeghị akara njedebe ọ bụla, retry ahụ na-edobe oke kacha nta dị larịị ahụ n’enweghị mgbanwe. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge a na-eji anata body nzaghachi zuru ezu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | Oge njikọ TCP si amalite ruo mgbe emechara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Oge socket keep-alive na-anọ n’enweghị ọrụ tupu ọ kwụsị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge nkwụsị proxy mkpịsị aka TLS (wreq-js).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Na-amachi oge ruo byte mbụ na ahụ nke njem TLS-mkpịsị-aka wreq-js kpọmkwem; `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` naanị ya enweghị ike ịchọpụta ahụ kwụsịrị, n’ihi na ọ na-akwụsị ichere ozugbo isi okwu rutere (#12656). Oge nkwụsị na-akagbu onye na-agụ wreq ma laghachi na onye na-ekesa direct/proxy; `0` na-ewepụ onye nche a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | Maka onye na-eme OpenCode naanị, nakwa naanị mgbe ọkọlọtọ atụmatụ `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` dị ọrụ (ndabara ya bụ gbanyụọ): na-amachi ichere byte mbụ nke ahụ sitere na nzaghachi Responses a na-asọpụta mgbe isi okwu ya rutere (#13484). Oghere Responses na-amalite na `response.created`, yabụ ịgbachi nkịtị gafere windo a bụ nkwụsị: a na-eme ka akaụntụ ahụ dajụọ, a na-atụgharịkwa arịrịọ ahụ gaa na akaụntụ na-esote otu ugboro; nkwụsị nke abụọ na-ada ozugbo. `0` na-ewepụ nchebe ahụ ọbụlagodi mgbe ọkọlọtọ ahụ dị ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`            | `0` (= off)                                                       | Maka onye na-eme OpenCode naanị: na-amachi ichere isi okwu nzaghachi sitere n’elu na oku Responses a na-asọpụta mgbe akaụntụ ọzọ ka dị — arịrịọ nọ n’ahịrị na-akwụsị ichere ma gaa n’ihu kama ijide windo isi okwu niile. Oku Responses a na-asọpụta naanị ka o metụtara (oghere Responses na-amalite na `response.created`, yabụ ịgbachi nkịtị ebe ahụ bụ ahịrị, ọ bụghị imepụta); mmejuputa nkata na oku ndị na-asụghị na-asọpụta emetụtaghị, windo ahụ na-ebelata naanị oke mmalite fetch dị irè, ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-agbatị ya. Uru a tụrụ aro mgbe a na-eme ka ọ dị ọrụ: `30000`. `0` na-ewepụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS` | `2`                                                               | Maka onye na-eme OpenCode naanị: ugboro ole kwa arịrịọ oke ichere isi okwu dị n’elu nwere ike isi mee ka ọ gaa na akaụntụ na-esote. Akaụntụ ikpeazụ fọdụrụ na-ejide windo isi okwu zuru ezu mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                      | `false`                                                           | Maka onye na-eme OpenCode naanị: debe arịrịọ ahụ n’ebe nchekwa ya na heartbeat mgbe `429` na-agafe nwa oge ugboro ugboro (ma ọ bụ akara ọhụrụ nke nrụgide ọdọ mmiri), wee kpọgharịa otu akụkụ nwere oke nke ihe ruru akaụntụ 3 n’usoro kama iziga ya n’aka ìgwè niile (#13924). A gbanyụrụ ya na ndabara: `429` ọ bụla na-atụgharị gaa na akaụntụ na-esote kpọmkwem dịka ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_REPLAY_SERVE_SETASIDE`           | `false`                                                           | Maka onye na-eme OpenCode naanị: mgbe akaụntụ ọ bụla dị njikere mgbe oge jụụ gasịrị n’akụkụ replay park-and-resume wepụrụ site na ncheta jụrụ proxy, jeere ha ozi agbanyeghị kama ịghara replay ihe ọ bụla (#14851, nsonazụ nke #14750). A gbanyụrụ ya na ndabara: akụkụ replay ahụ na-abụ ihe efu n’ọnọdụ ahụ, dịka ọ dị tupu #14750.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                  | `false`                                                           | Naanị nkata na-enuba: mgbe ahụ upstream mbụ kwụsịrị tupu o wepụta ihe omume a pụrụ iji, mee otu mbọ nke abụọ nwere oke site n'otu ụzọ routing ahụ, jiri mmefu ego readiness ahụ, na-enweghị ntaramahụhụ akaụntụ. Akwụsịghị ya na ndabara: ahụ mbụ kwụsịrị na-eme ka arịrịọ ahụ daa n’enweghị retry.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                        | `false`                                                           | Naanị OpenCode executor: mgbe 429 sitere n'aka provider e kenyere n'egress bucket n'akaụntụ na-enweghị proxy n'okpuru ọnọdụ pool ambient, rịọ connection pool ka o họrọ onye ọzọ maka mbọ ọzọ kama ịnwale otu adreesị egress ahụ ọzọ. Ọ na-ahazi n'usoro, ọ naghị ewepụ: pool gwụchara na-aga n'ihu na omume dị ugbu a. Akwụsịghị ya na ndabara: 429 ọ bụla na-atụgharị gaa na akaụntụ na-esote dịka ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`              | _(unset)_                                                         | Naanị OpenCode executor: gbanwee ụzọ nke pool-strain marker a na-agụ tupu ịkwụsịtụ (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, ndabara `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Marker ọhụrụ na-eme ka a kwụsịtụ n’enweghị ịgụ ọnụ ọgụgụ ọzọ; nke na-adịghị adị ma ọ bụ nke kubie ume na-alaghachi na burst counter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Oge ngafe proxy hop maka arịrịọ bridge `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `FIRECRAWL_BASE_URL`                            | `https://api.firecrawl.dev`                                       | Tụọ Firecrawl web-fetch executor gaa n'ihe atụ self-hosted (igodo API bụ nhọrọ mgbe ọ nọ na mpụga igwe ojii).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `FIRECRAWL_TIMEOUT_MS`                          | `30000`                                                           | Oge ngafe kwa-arịrịọ maka Firecrawl web-fetch executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `TAVILY_BASE_URL`                               | _(unset)_                                                         | URL ntọala maka Tavily `/usage` quota fetcher (Provider Limits); a na-eleghara ya anya mgbe ọ na-atụ aka na `api.tavily.com`. Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, ọ na-eji URL ntọala nke connection ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `300000`                                                          | Oge nkwụsị arịrịọ sava n'ozuzu maka àkwà mmiri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                                                           | Oge izipu nkụnye ndị azịza site na àkwà mmiri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`        | `5000`                                                            | Oge nkwụsị ịnọ na njikọ na-adịghị arụ ọrụ nke àkwà mmiri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `0`                                                               | Oge nkwụsị socket nkịtị (0 = agbanyụrụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `SHUTDOWN_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | `30000`                                                           | Oge ndidi mgbe SIGTERM/SIGINT gasịrị tupu mmanye ịpụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEFAULT_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                                          | A na-eji ya dị ka nhọrọ ndabere site na `src/shared/utils/fetchTimeout.ts` mgbe edoghị `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `8000`                                                            | Oge nkwụsị (ms) maka nhazi ndị e debere ụzọ `validationRead` na `modelsProbe` dị na `src/shared/network/safeOutboundFetch.ts`. Welie ya maka ebe njedebe na-eji nwayọọ (Cerebras, Cloudflare AI, Groq) iji gbochie ịgbanwegharị ugboro ugboro n'etiti ọnọdụ arụ ọrụ na njehie na bọọdụ njikwa. Ọ na-alaghachi na 8000ms maka ụkpụrụ na-adịghị mma (<1000) ma ọ bụ ụkpụrụ na-abụghị ọnụọgụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`              | `25000`                                                           | Ogeysi fetch a kapịrị ọnụ maka relay na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). Relay kwụsịrị ịza ga-adarịrị tupu oge njedebe onye ahịa/agent (~30s), ka ndị na-akpọ ya hụ ọdịda kapịrị ọnụ nke relay kama oge njedebe upstream zuru ụwa ọnụ. A kpachiri ya na `29000` ka ọ na-arụ ọrụ mgbe niile tupu nke ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                    | `10`                                                              | Oge ichere n'etiti nnwale ọzọ nke a na-ekekọrịta maka ụzọ direct/relay/proxy ndị na-anwale ọzọ otu ugboro na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = nwalee ọzọ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                                                           | Oge njedebe arịrịọ wreq-js nke obodo (`claudeTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLAUDE_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Oge mgbakwunye nke JS hard-deadline zuru oke, a na-agbakwunye ya n’elu oge njedebe nke obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
