| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `30000`                                                           | Oge njedebe arịrịọ wreq-js nke obodo (`perplexityTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Oge mgbakwunye nke JS hard-deadline zuru oke, a na-agbakwunye ya n’elu oge njedebe nke obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PPLX_SEARCH_HINT`                    | `0` (gbanyụọ)                                                     | Na-agbakwunye "Ị nwere ọchụchọ weebụ wuru n’ime. Jiri nsonaazụ ọchụchọ zaa ajụjụ ozugbo." na ozi sistemụ onye na-akpọ ya (`perplexity-web/protocol.ts`). A na-agbanyụ ya na ndabara — Perplexity na-eme ọchụchọ n’agbanyeghị, ahịrịokwu ahụ na-abanyekwa na nzaghachi dị ka nkọwa meta maka ndị ahịa koodu. Tọọ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji weghachite ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`                 | `60000`                                                           | Oge a maara n’oge maka arịrịọ wreq-js nke ala (`grokTlsClient.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Oge mgbakwunye JS siri ike zuru oke nke a na-agbakwunye n’elu oge a maara n’oge nke ala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                                           | Oge a maara n’oge maka arịrịọ wreq-js nke ala (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` na-ebuli ya maka arịrịọ ọ bụla ruo `180000` maka mmepụta ogologo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Oge mgbakwunye JS siri ike zuru oke nke a na-agbakwunye n’elu oge a maara n’oge nke ala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Pool Playwright browser nkekọrịta maka mkparịta ụka web-cookie nke browser na-akwado (`browserPool.ts`); tọọ ya na `off` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ ọnụọgụ abụọ `obscura` nke a na-eji dị ka engine mbụ site na pool browser na onye na-eme Cloudflare Playground (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); a na-achọpụta ya na-akpaghị aka site na PATH sistemụ mgbe a na-edoghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(unset)_                                                         | Tụnye aka na Obscura na-agba ọsọ ugbu a (`http://host:port`) kama ịmalite otu; modul ahụ abụghị onye nwe usoro ahụ (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Port a kapịrị ọnụ maka `obscura serve` a malitere; a ga-ahọrọ port efu na-akpaghị aka mgbe edoghị ya (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Tinye arịrịọ nkata web-cookie n'okporo ụzọ nke ihe nchọgharị na-akwado (`browserBackedChat.ts`); jiri `1` mee ka ọ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | URL ntọala maka onye na-arụ ọrụ Kimi Web (international kimi.ai Connect-RPC) (`kimi-web.ts`); gbanwee ya naanị maka endpoints mirror/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Endpoint nkata zuru ezu maka onye na-arụ ọrụ Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(a na-achọpụta akpaghị aka)_                                     | Ụzọ gaa na executable Chrome/Edge nke sistemụ maka nbanye Adobe Firefly mmekọrịta site n'ihe nchọgharị (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); ọ na-edochi nchọpụta akpaghị aka nke dabere na OS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _.build/ standalone output_                                       | Ndochi n'oge build maka ndekọ mmepụta standalone nke usoro post-build colocation na-eji (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); ọ bụ ngwá ọrụ build, ọ bụghị runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

Mgbalị combo target na-eketa timeout arịrịọ upstream e kpebiri (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ma ọ bụ
`REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe ọ na-enye fetch default). Tọọ `targetTimeoutMs` n'ime combo,
combo defaults, ma ọ bụ provider override naanị iji mee ka combo fallback dị ngwa; a na-egbochi ụkpụrụ ndị karịrị
timeout upstream dị ugbu a ka ha ghara ịkarị timeout upstream.

`comboTimeoutMs` bụ mmefu ego elekere zuru ezu dị iche maka combo niile, n'ofe failover targets.
Hapụ ya ka ọ ghara ịdị ma ọ bụ tọọ ya na `0` iji mee ka iteration ghara inwe oke (nkwụsị hang siri ike nke nkeji 10 dị na
`comboPredicates.ts` ka na-arụ ọrụ). Ụkpụrụ dị mma na-edochi
safety net ahụ maka combo ahụ. Debe `comboTimeoutMs` ka ọ dị ogologo karịa `targetTimeoutMs` ka failover
ka nwee oge mgbe target mbụ jiri nwayọọ zaghachi.

### Ọnụ-Ihe Nkwụsị Circuit Breaker

Nhazi circuit breaker n'ọkwa provider. Defaults na-egosipụta ụkpụrụ a tụrụ nha nke a na-eji kemgbe v3.6 maka njikọ 500+.

| Mgbanwe                                                       | Ndabara   | Faịlụ Isi                                            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Oke ọdịda na-esochi onwe ha maka ndị na-enye ọrụ OAuth tupu ihe mgbochi amalite ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Oge windo mweghachi (ms) maka ihe mgbochi onye na-enye ọrụ OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Oke ọdịda na-esochi onwe ha maka ndị na-enye ọrụ API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Oge windo mweghachi (ms) maka ihe mgbochi onye na-enye ọrụ API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Oke ọdịda na-esochi onwe ha maka ndị na-enye ọrụ dị n'ime mpaghara (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Oge windo mweghachi (ms) maka ihe mgbochi onye na-enye ọrụ dị n'ime mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe mgbochi n'ọkwa onye na-enye ọrụ: ọdịda ole a na-anabata n'ime windo tupu onye na-enye ọrụ OAuth niile abanye n'oge jụrụ oyi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe mgbochi n'ọkwa onye na-enye ọrụ: windo (ms) na-agagharị maka ịgụ ọdịda nke ndị na-enye ọrụ OAuth.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe mgbochi n'ọkwa onye na-enye ọrụ: oge jụrụ oyi (ms) mgbe oke onye na-enye ọrụ OAuth ruru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`      | `5`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Onye na-enye ọrụ OAuth na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`     | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọtụtụ mmụba kachasị elu nke onye na-enye ọrụ OAuth na-eji maka ịrị elu resetTimeout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT`   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Onye na-enye ọrụ OAuth na-ebuli ọkwa mgbe usoro mmeghe ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe mgbochi n'ọkwa onye na-enye ọrụ: ọdịda ole a na-anabata n'ime windo tupu onye na-enye ọrụ API-key niile abanye n'oge jụrụ oyi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe mgbochi n'ọkwa onye na-enye ọrụ: windo (ms) na-agagharị maka ịgụ ọdịda nke ndị na-enye ọrụ API-key.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`              | `600000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe mgbochi n'ọkwa onye na-enye ọrụ: oge jụrụ oyi (ms) mgbe oke onye na-enye ọrụ API-key ruru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`    | `7`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Onye na-enye ọrụ API-key na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`   | `4`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ọtụtụ mmụba kachasị elu nke onye na-enye ọrụ API-key na-eji maka ịrị elu resetTimeout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT` | `3`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Onye na-enye ọrụ API-key na-ebuli ọkwa mgbe usoro mmeghe ruru ọnụọgụ a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta ọrụ: ọnụọgụ ọdịda tupu onye na-eweta ọrụ mpaghara dum abanye n'oge nkwụsịtụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta ọrụ: windo na-atụgharị maka ịgụ ọdịda (ms) maka ndị na-eweta ọrụ mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                       | Ihe nkwụsị n'ọkwa onye na-eweta ọrụ: oge nkwụsịtụ (ms) ozugbo onye na-eweta ọrụ mpaghara ruru oke ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`                               | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts`                 | Oge nkwụsịtụ kwa igodo (ms) nke ntụgharị igodo API ọtụtụ na-etinye mgbe upstream 429 enweghị `Retry-After` (#14573). Uru na-erughị ma ọ bụ hà na efu, ma ọ bụ uru na-abụghị ọnụọgụ, na-alaghachi na nke ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_AVALANCHE_PROTECTION_ENABLED`                      | `false`   | `open-sse/services/accountFallback/antiAvalanche.ts` | Nchedo mgbochi avalanche nke a na-ahọrọ iji rụọ ọrụ: naanị `true` akọwapụtara nke ọma na-eme ka ọ rụọ ọrụ. Mgbe ọ na-arụ ọrụ, 429 nke kpọrọ oke tokens kwa nkeji (yana 400 nke context-overflow) na-akwụsị failover na-agbasawanye n'ofe igodo ndị fọdụrụ nke onye na-eweta ọrụ. 429 nke quota gwụchara na-aga n'ihu na failover n'agbanyeghị etu prompt ahụ hà, ebe HTTP 413 anaghị emetụta (ọ na-aga n'ihu na omume retry-on-another-account nke #13350). |
| `OMNIROUTE_AVALANCHE_BURST_COOLDOWN_MS`                       | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/accountFallback/antiAvalanche.ts` | Model-mkpọchi cooldown (ms) etinyere mgbe TPM 429 nche ọkụ, kama mkpọchi na-arị elu. Ntọgharị elu dị ogologo na-emeri mgbe niile. Ụkpụrụ na-abụghị nke ziri ezi ma ọ bụ na-abụghị ọnụọgụ na-adaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                         | Ọkwa backoff nke a na-ewere onye na-eweta ọrụ nke pin cache-context dị ka onye na-arịa ọrịa na-adịgide adịgide, wee tufuo pin ahụ maka failover.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                         | Windo mgbochi mgbanwe ugboro ugboro (ms) nke na-anabata oge nkwụsịtụ dị mkpirikpi tupu atụfu pin cache-context.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

### Ọnọdụ

| Ọnọdụ                                  | Nhazi                                                    |
| -------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ịmepụta koodu na-ewe ogologo oge**   | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (nkeji 15)                   |
| **Ịkekọrịta oke ogologo ndụ iyi**      | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (nkeji 21)            |
| **Ịkwụsị ngwa ngwa maka API mmepụta**  | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                      |
| **Modelụ na-eche echiche ogologo oge** | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (nkeji 5 n'etiti chunks) |

## 16. Ndekọ

Sistemụ ndekọ na-ede ma na stdout ma na faịlụ ndekọ ndị a na-atụgharịgharị. A na-agụ nhazi niile site na `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Mgbanwe                                   | Ndabara                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Ọkwa ndekọ kacha nta: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Ọdịdị mmepụta: `text` (nke mmadụ nwere ike ịgụ) ma ọ bụ `json` (ahaziri ahazi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Dee ndekọ na faịlụ yana stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Ụzọ faịlụ ndekọ (n'ihe metụtara mgbọrọgwụ prọjektị ma ọ bụ `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Ogo faịlụ kachasị tupu ntụgharị. Ọ na-anabata: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ma ọ bụ bytes nkịtị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edowe faịlụ ndekọ ngwa ndị a tụgharịworo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Ọnụọgụ ndabere faịlụ ndekọ ndị a tụgharịworo kachasị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edowe ndenye ndekọ arịrịọ/oku na ebe nchekwa data.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Ọnụọgụ ndenye ndekọ oku kachasị na buffer dị na ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kachasị na tebụl SQLite `call_logs` tupu ebelata ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(edoghị ya)_              | Manye ndekọ arịrịọ zuru ezu ka ọ gbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ, na-akagbu ntọala dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (1 elekere)      | Ogologo oge kachasị maka ndenye ndekọ arịrịọ nọ n'ọrụ ma bụrụ nke a hapụrụ tupu nhicha n'ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MAX_PENDING_DETAILS`                     | `5000`                     | Nkọwa kachasị na-echere (integer karịrị efu); oke ọnụ ọgụgụ: mgbe a na-ehichapụ; oke byte/map: mgbe a na-etinye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Chekwaa iberibe stream na pipeline artifacts mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Nhọrọ nke onye ọrụ ga-eme (`true`) — agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara iji chekwaa ohere diski.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Ogo artifact ndekọ oku pipeline kachasị na KB mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kachasị na tebụl SQLite `proxy_logs` tupu ebelata ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Tinye IP ndị ahịa/egress na prefix akaụntụ na ndekọ console `[ProxyEgress]`. Ihe ndekọ proxy-log nke dashboard/ebe nchekwa data na-edowe nkọwa niile.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_LOG_FIRST_CHUNK_TIMING`            | `false`                    | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` ma ọ bụ `"1"` iji dekọọ `first_chunk_ms` (site na mbido izipu ruo na byte mbụ bara uru nke body) n'ahịrị proxy-log. Ọ na-etinye body upstream ọ bụla n'ime stream passthrough umengwụ ma na-eme patch n'ahịrị ahụ ozugbo byte ahụ rutere; agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara ka response gafee n'enweghị mgbanwe. |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (1 nkeji)          | Ugboro ole `src/lib/logRotation.ts` na-enyochagharị nha faịlụ ndekọ nọ n'ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Ogologo string kachasị a na-edowe na chat log artifacts (ndabara bụ 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Ọnụọgụ ihe array a na-edowe site na ngwụcha mgbe a na-ebipụ chat log payloads.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Omimi ntinye kachasị tupu ebipụ chat log payloads.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Object keys kachasị a na-edowe na chat log payloads (0 = enweghị oke).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Nha body arịrịọ/response dum (KB) tupu e jiri nchịkọta nkịtị dochie ya kama clone zuru ezu. Welie nke a ma ọ bụrụ na mkparịta ụka agentic ogologo na-egosi placeholder kama ozi ndị ahụ n'ezie na dashboard.                                                                                                                     |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Mgbe ọ bụ true, `serializeArtifactForStorage` na-amafere mbipụ dabere na nha. Maka debug naanị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

## 17. Nkwalite Ebe Nchekwa

| Agbanwe                                      | Ndabara                                                                                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`                        | _auto_ (bare metal); **`1024` na foto Docker**                                                              | **A na-atụ aro** oke V8 heap Docker/standalone (MB). Mgbe a na-esetịghị ya, a na-atụle ya n'ụzọ na-agbanwe agbanwe (~35% nke RAM sistemu, a machibidoro ya na `[512, 4096]`); `512` bụ naanị ala kacha ala mgbe a na-apụghị ịgụ ngụkọta ebe nchekwa. Na `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), a na-agbakwụnye uru **a kapịrị ọnụ** dị ka `--max-old-space-size` ma ọ **na-merie** flag NODE_OPTIONS heap na-emegbu ya (V8 last-flag). `omniroute serve` ka na-ahọrọ NODE_OPTIONS heap dị adị (#5238). Esetịla ha abụọ na ọnụ ọgụgụ dị iche iche — usoro ahụ na-edekọ ịdọ aka nántị na-akpọ aha uru abụọ ahụ na onye mmeri. **Foto Docker gọọmentị na-esetị `1024` mgbe niile, yabụ ntụle anaghị arụ ọrụ ebe ahụ.** Coding-agent `/v1/responses` chọrọ `8192`–`12288` gbakwụnye ohere cgroup — lee [Ntuziaka Docker — runtime RAM](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `OMNIROUTE_RSS_PRESSURE_MB`                  | _auto_ (`max(3072, 2 × OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB)`; ọ na-adịghị arụ ọrụ mgbe a na-esetịghị `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`) | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Fuse process-RSS zuru oke (MB) maka ebe nchekwa native/external nke V8 `heapUsed` na-apụghị ịhụ; n'elu ya, arịrị ọhụrụ na-enweta 503 `resource_pressure`. RSS na-adịkarịghị adata mgbe mmụba gasịrị, yabụ jikọta ya na `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`. |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`                      | `50`                                                                                                        | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke ndenye system prompt e cache-ara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`                     | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                                                            | Ogo ngụkọta kacha elu nke cache prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`                        | `300000` (5 nkeji)                                                                                          | TTL nke ndenye cache prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`                    | `100`                                                                                                       | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke nzaghachi temperature=0 e cache-ara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`                   | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                                                            | Ogo ngụkọta kacha elu nke cache semantic.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`                      | `1800000` (30 nkeji)                                                                                        | TTL nke ndenye cache semantic.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_INTERVAL_MS` | `1800000` (30 min)                                                                                          | Oge dị n'etiti nhicha SQLite. `0` na-agbanyụ ịrụ ya n'oge mmalite na ịrụ ya kwa oge; uru ndị ọzọ dị n'okpuru `60000` na-alaghachi na ndabara. A na-ejedebe uru karịrị `2147483647` ms na oge igbu oge kachasị nke timer Node, ka overflow ghara ịkpata polling kwa 1ms. Oge mbụ ọ na-agba bụ mgbe 1s gasịrị; timers anaghị eme ka process nọgide dị ndụ, ọrụ abụọ anaghịkwa ekpuchi ibe ha n'oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_BATCH_SIZE`  | `2000`                                                                                                      | Ahịrị kwa DELETE nhicha SQLite, e jedebere na `[100, 50000]`; na-enye event loop ohere ịchị n'etiti batch dị iche iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_PRUNE_MAX_BATCHES` | `50`                                                                                                        | Oke batch DELETE a na-ekekọrịta kwa oge ọrụ, n'etiti ngafe iwepụ ihe gafere oge na ngafe nhọrọ nke oke ahịrị, e jedebere na `[1, 10000]`. Ọrụ fọdụrụ na-aga n'ihu n'oge ọrụ ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_ROWS`          | `0`                                                                                                         | Oke ahịrị SQLite nke nhọrọ (`0` = enweghị). Mgbe ihichapụchara ahịrị nwere `expires_at <=` akara oge ISO nke mmalite ọrụ, ọ na-ewepụ ndị kacha ochie site na `created_at`, emesia `rowid`, gụnyere ahịrị ndị ka dị irè, n'ime oke batch a na-ekekọrịta. A na-ebipụ ọnụ ọgụgụ ka ha bụrụ ọnụọgụ zuru ezu (ruo oke ọnụọgụ zuru ezu dị nchebe). Uru tọgbọ chakoo, ndị abụghị ọnụọgụ, ndị na-adịghị finite ma ọ bụ ndị dị n'okpuru efu maka variable ọ bụla n'ime anọ nke nhicha na-alaghachi na ndabara ha; a na-agbakọta irighiri ọnụọgụ ala. Nhicha anaghị agbanwe TTL, LRU dị na memory ma ọ bụ omume vector cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                | `5`                                                                                                         | Mgbọrọgwụ corpus mpaghara nke na-edobe index dị ndụ na nchekwa n'otu oge (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: na oke ahụ, a na-ewepụ index nke mgbọrọgwụ kacha nso e ji mee ihe n'oge na-adịbeghị anya ma wughachie ya na ajụjụ ya na-esote. A machibidoro ya na kacha nta nke `1`; uru na-abụghị ọnụ ọgụgụ na-alaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`                         | `50`                                                                                                        | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke mmemme stream na-adịbeghị anya na buffer nlele ndụ Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`                     | _(a na-esetịghị ya)_                                                                                        | Nchịkọta ogologo context zuru ụwa ọnụ (tokens) maka onye na-enye ọ bụla nke na-enweghị agbanwe `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`. Mgbe e setịpụrụ ya, ọ **na-anọchi** uru catalog, nchịkọta dashboard na registry maka ndị na-enye ndị ahụ; mgbe a na-esetịghị ya, OmniRoute na-eji yinye ya e wuru n'ime, nke ikpeazụ ya bụ `128000`. Lee [Ogologo context per-provider](#per-provider-context-length-context_length_provider).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`                         | `100`                                                                                                       | Ohere token mgbakwunye e chekwara mgbe a na-esocha oke ojiji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

### Ogologo context per-provider (`CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`)

`open-sse/services/contextManager.ts` (`getEnvOverride()` / `resolveTokenLimit()`) na-agụkwa ụdị
**per-provider** nke a rụrụ aha ya site na ID onye na-enye na oge ọrụ, yabụ a na-apụghị
edepụta ya ahịrị site na ahịrị na tebụl dị n'elu ma ọ bụ na `.env.example`.

**Aha.** `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`, ebe `<PROVIDER>` bụ provider ID e dere n'ụkwụ edemede ukwu (uppercase), ebe a na-eji `_` dochie mkpụrụedemede ọ bụla nke na-abụghị `A-Z` / `0-9`. Provider ID ahụ bụ nke izugbe mgbe e doziri alias (akụkụ `provider` nke eriri `provider/model` mgbe e doziri alias dị mkpirikpi). Ọdụ (nodes) OpenAI- ma ọ bụ Anthropic-compatible ndị e mepụtara omenala na-eji node ID ha mepụtara, ya mere UUID bụ akụkụ nke aha mgbanwe ahụ. Ọnụọgụ ahụ bụ ọnụọgụ zuru eke dị mma nke tokens; ihe ọ bụla ọzọ (oghere efu, `0`, nke ọdịda, nke na-abụghị ọnụọgụ) a na-ahapụ ya, ka nhazi ahụ gaa n'usoro na-esote.

| Provider ID                                                 | Aha mgbanwe                                                                |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| agentrouter                                                 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER                                                 |
| openrouter                                                  | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENROUTER                                                  |
| openai-compatible-chat-0f8e2c1a-5b7d-4e3f-9a6c-2d1b8e4f7a90 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_CHAT_0F8E2C1A_5B7D_4E3F_9A6C_2D1B8E4F7A90 |

**Ọnọdụ mbụ.** Maka ihe mmekọ `provider/model`, usoro mbụ nke na-enye ọnụọgụ bụ nke na-eri:

1. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>` maka provider ahụ.
2. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT` (mbochichi gburugburu zuru ụwa ọnụ).
3. Windo nke model ọ bụla: mbochichi ọdịnaya e chekwara (e tọrọ na dashboard, ma ọ bụ reconciler nchọpụta pin-nyere ya naanị ya, lee `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`), ma ọ bụghị otu a, ọnụọgụ katalọgụ e jikọtara / models.dev.
4. `defaultContextLength` nke registry provider ahụ.
5. Nyocha aha model (`claude` 200000, `gemini` 1000000, `gpt` / `o1` / `o3` / `o4` / `codex` 400000).
6. Ọnọdụ mbụ e wuru n'ime maka provider ọ bụla, mgbe ahụ `128000`.

Usoro gburugburu abụọ ahụ na-emetụta **provider niile**: model ọ bụla nke provider ahụ na-enweta oke otu ahụ, ha na-eri mbochichi dashboard nke model ọ bụla. Mgbe naanị otu model nke provider chọrọ windo dị iche, jiri mbochichi nke model ọ bụla nọ na dashboard kama.

**Oke metụtara.** Usoro a na-akwalite nyocha windo ọdịnaya n'oge arịrịọ (jụ `context_length_exceeded` / "Input exceeds context window" n'ime `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`), mmefu mbẹda prompt na oke ọdịnaya combo. `context_length` nke `GET /v1/models` na-akwasa nwere ike ịbụ ihe si na metadata katalọgụ ma ọ bụ registry, a naghị ekwe nkwa na ọ ga-egosipụta mbochichi gburugburu ahụ.

**Ọmụmaatụ.** Provider nke model ya na-anabata windo token 1,050,000 ebe katalọgụ na-akọ 128,000:

```dotenv
CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER=1050000
```

Malitegharịa server mgbe ị gbanwere ya: a na-agụ ọnụọgụ ahụ site na gburugburu usoro server. Na **ngwa desktọpụ**, server na-arụ ọrụ dị ka usoro nwa (child process), ọ hụghị mgbanwe sistemu e tọrọ mgbe e malitere ngwa ahụ; tinye akara ahụ na faịlụ `.env` nke ngwa ahụ na-ebu (usoro ọchọ dị na [Ntuziaka Electron: Ọchọ faịlụ gburugburu](../guides/ELECTRON_GUIDE.md#environment-file-lookup)), wee mechie ngwa ahụ kpamkpam ma malite ya ọzọ.

### Mbẹda

| Mgbanwe                               | Nke mbụ | Nkọwa                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | e tọghị | Tụkwasị `.rtk/filters.json` nke ọrụ ahụ obi na-enweghị hash `.rtk/trust.json`. Jiri ya naanị na mmepe obodo a na-achịkwa. |

### Injin Echeta (atụmatụ 21)

### Ọnụ ego event-loop nke echeta, nkà, na mmelite token (#10349)

OmniRoute bụ **otu usoro Node**. Mwepụ/inyeghachị echeta, itinye nkà, na mmelite token provider na-agba ọsọ na **otu event loop ahụ** dị ka `GET /healthz` na dashboard. Ha abụghị worker thread.

| Ọrụ                       | Koodu                              | Nke mbụ                                     | Njikwa onye ọrụ                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Mwepụ / inyeghachị echeta | `src/lib/memory/`                  | Dashboard **memoryEnabled** (nke mbụ bụ on) | Gbanyụọ **Settings → Memory**. Enweghị kill switch env dị iche karịa ịgbanyụ atụmatụ ahụ na ntọala.                        |
| Itinye nkà                | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | Dashboard **skillsEnabled** (nke mbụ bụ on) | Gbanyụọ **Settings → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Njikwa sandbox dị n'okpuru na-eke oke ọrụ naanị mgbe e tinyewo ya. |
| Mmelite token             | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | On maka providers OAuth/web e jikọtara      | Kwụsịnjikọ provider ahụ ma ọ bụ hapụ ka tokens dị irè; enweghị env `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` taa.                                  |

Ọ bụrụ na `/healthz` na-ebu nwayọ na igwe dị jụụ, gbanyụọ echeta + nkà mbụ, wee lelee ibu katalọgụ/mbẹda (#10303, #9685). Atụmatụ ndị a na-ahapụ ohere n'ebe `await` mana ha ka na-asọ mpi maka otu eri (thread) ahụ.

Oyi embedding, ụlọọrụ vector na njikwa reranking maka usoro ncheta na-adịgide adịgide (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Mgbanwe                              | Nhazi dabara                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (nkeji 5)          | TTL maka cache embedding dị n'ime nchekwa (kwa signature source/model/dim).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Ọnụ ọgụgụ entries LRU kacha elu echekwara na cache embedding.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | HF repo id maka pipeline MiniLM mpaghara opt-in `@huggingface/transformers` (~23 MB int8, ~400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | HF repo id maka embedder lookup-table potion/Model2Vec kwụrụ otu ebe. A na-ebudata ya nanị mgbe a chọrọ ya n'ime ndekọ cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Ndekọ ebe a na-eche faịlụ model potion kwụrụ otu ebe. Ọ dabeere n'okpuru `DATA_DIR` mgbe a hazighị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Ịdochie base URL Hugging Face Hub nke `staticPotion.ts` na-eji (dị ka mirror endpoint maka ntọlite air-gapped).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Top-K dabara nke `sqlite-vec` brute-force vector search dị n'ime `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts` na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Kọnstantị Reciprocal Rank Fusion `k` maka wepụta hybrid FTS5 + vector (usoro sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Oghere nnwale/nchọpụta na `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): mgbe ọ bụ `true`, ọ na-amanye vector store ka ọ bụrụ `null` (na-emeghe gburugburu ebe cloud/WASM na-enweghị `sqlite-vec`), na-ebelata wepụta nchekwa ka ọ bụrụ ọchọ ọkwa okwu FTS5. Ahapụla ya ka a hazighị ya na mmepụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(a hazighị)_               | Igodo API maka backend Notion (nke preset backend a maara nke `genericBackend.ts` na-eji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | Base URL maka Notion API (enwere ike ịdochie ya maka ndị ọzọ Notion self-hosted).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(a hazighị)_               | Igodo API maka Obsidian Vault backend (nke preset backend a maara nke `genericBackend.ts` na-eji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | Base URL maka Obsidian Vault API (enwere ike ịdochie ya maka vault dị anya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | Ịgba ọkụ isi nke TV6 typed memory decay. **Opt-in (n'ụzọ dabara gbanyụrụ)** — sweep ahụ **na-ehichapụ** memories rụrụ arụ. Mgbe ọ gbanyụrụ, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` bụ telemetry dị ọcha na ọ dịghị ihe a na-ehichapụ mgbe ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (ụbọchị) mgbe nke ahụ memory `episodic` a naghị eji eme ihe na-arụ arụ. `0` na-eme ka episodic nwee mgbochi. Ụdị durable (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) na-enwe mgbochi mgbe niile. Elekere arụ arụ na-ebu `last_accessed_at` n'ala ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Memory etinyere `>=` ugboro a na-enwe mgbochi megide arụ arụ n'agbanyeghị ụdị. `0` na-agbanyụ mgbochi ohere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (agbanyụrụ)             | Interval (sekọnd) maka sweep arụ arụ kwa oge nhọrọ na `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/a hazighị = ọ dịghị sweep kwa oge. Opt-in ugboro abụọ: ọ chọkwara `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(a hazighị)_               | ID providers kewapụrụ na-comma (anaghị ele ukwu/nta anya) nke na-anabata ozi `system` **nanị na index 0** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Maka ndị a, splice nchekwa nke dị n'etiti array nke cache na-echebe adịghị nchebe na mkparịta ụka multi-turn, ya mere a na-ejikọ/tinye nchekwa n'ihu dị ka ozi system nke mbụ. Ọ dabeere na nanị `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo`; gbatie ya maka endpoints OpenAI-compatible self-hosted (dị ka Qwen3.5+/3.6) nke template chats ha na-amanye otu mgbochi ozi-system-naanị-n'ihu ahụ. |

### Ihe Nlereanya Docker RAM dị ala

`128` bụ dashboard naanị. Agents nka na-ede koodu na heap a na-enwe `FATAL ERROR` n'oge `/v1/responses` ogologo. Ejila ihe nlereanya a mee ihe dị ka gateway Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

## 18. Mmekọrịta Ọnụahịa

Mmekọrịta data ọnụahịa ụdịdị na-akpaghị aka site na isi mmalite mpụga.

| Mgbanwe                 | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta        | Nkọwa                                            |
| ----------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `PRICING_SYNC_ENABLED`  | `false`       | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Mmekọrịta ọnụahịa oge ọbụla a na-ahọrọ ịgbalite. |
| `PRICING_SYNC_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Oge dị n'etiti mmekọrịta na sekọnd.              |
| `PRICING_SYNC_SOURCES`  | `litellm`     | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Isi mmalite data ndị rikoma kewara.              |
