## 24. Ebe Nnwale Skills (v3.8.0+)

Oke na njikwa nchekwa ndị a na-etinye n'ọrụ mgbe usoro Skills (`src/lib/skills/`) na-eme akpaaka ndị ọrụ kọwara n'ime gburugburu e kewapụrụ iche.

| Mgbanwe                           | Ndabara                                                       | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                                                | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke oge mgbidi elekere maka mmezu nke ọ bụla nke koodu skill n'ebe nnwale e kewapụrụ iche. Ọ bụ oke siri ike; a ga-akwụsị ihe ọ bụla gafere ya.                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(ga-eji `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Oke oge maka nhazi mmezu skill n'ọkwa dị elu. Tọọ ya karịa `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` iji kwe ka usoro ọrụ nwere ọtụtụ nzọụkwụ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ọnụọgụ byte kachasị skill nwere ike ịgụ n'otu faịlụ ọ bụla dị n'ebe nnwale e kewapụrụ iche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                                             | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ọnụọgụ byte kachasị a ga-edekọ site na otu nzaghachi HTTP ọ bụla n'ime skill.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                                      | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke siri ike maka ọnụọgụ mkpụrụedemede stdout/stderr a na-eweghachite site na otu oku ebe nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tọọ `1`/`true` iji kwe ka njikọ netwọk na-apụ apụ si n'ime ebe nnwale. Na ndabara, ọ bụ **nke e kewapụrụ iche** maka nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(efu)_                                                       | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ndepụta oyiyi container ndị e nyere ikike maka mmezu n'ebe nnwale, nke e ji rikoma kewaa. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo, naanị ndabara arụnyere n'ime ka a ga-eji.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(ndabara arụnyere n'ime)_                                    | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Oyiyi container a na-eji mgbe a na-ebido ebe nnwale Docker. Dochie ya iji kpọgide oyiyi ntọala ahaziri iche ma sie ike n'ihe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                                        | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Runtime container maka ikewapụ skill n'ebe nnwale: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` na-ahọrọ runtime arụnyere kachasị mma dabere na OS nke host (Apple Container/OrbStack na macOS, WSL Container na Windows, Podman na Linux), ma jiri Docker ma ọ bụrụ na ndị ahụ adịghị. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Ịgbanye `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` na-emepe ụzọ ọpụpụ site na koodu skill ọ bụla. Jikọtara ya na `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` yana iwu `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy siri ike n'ime nrụnye ndị ọtụtụ ndị na-eji ọnụ.

---

## 25. Oke Ojiji nke Onye Na-enye Ọrụ, Ọwara, Ndabere & Runtime Ndị Ọzọ

Nkwụsị nkwado affinity nke nnọkọ Redis (naanị mgbe ahọpụtara ya; ndabara SQLite agbanwebeghị):

Ụlọ nchekwa affinity Redis ọ bụla na-anabata ihe karịrị arụmọrụ 8 na-echereghị; mgbe ikike juru, oku na-eji SQLite ozugbo, oghere ndị ahụ na-anọgide na-ejide ruo mgbe ọrụ Redis dị n’okpuru kwụsịrị, ọbụna mgbe oge njedebe onye na-akpọ oku nke 100 ms gafere.

- R1. Azịza Redis ọ bụla rutere n’oge maka ọrụ nnọkọ bụ nke a pụrụ ịdabere na ya: mgbanwe ndị a nabatara, CAS ndị a jụrụ ma ọ bụ furu efu, igodo ndị na-adịghị, nhichapụ igodo na-adịghị, na ihe kpatara ma ọ bụ enweghị ihe kpatara eviction na-ekpochapụ ahịrị fallback mpaghara nke nnọkọ ahụ n’otu oge, na-enweghị `await` n’etiti azịza na nhichapụ. Nhichapụ ahụ adịghị emerụ ahụ (n’ọnọdụ kacha njọ, a ga-ahọrọ ọzọ otu ugboro n’oge ọdịda ọzọ); ahịrị ochie adịghị mma.
- R2. Ọrụ nke oge ya agwụla ma ọ bụ nke nwere njehie wee dee fallback mpaghara na-edekọ kpọmkwem ihe o dere dị ka bytes ndị a serialize-rịrị. Ọ bụrụ na iwu Redis ya mechaa n’oge na-abịa, SQLite compare-and-delete nke na-eme n’otu oge na-ekpochapụ naanị ahịrị ahụ nwere bytes ndị ahụ kpọmkwem, ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-eji akaụntụ ma ọ bụ njikọ dakọtara. Ihe odide ọdịda ọhụrụ na-adị ndụ.
- R3. Eviction na-ewepụ pin mpaghara nke akaụntụ dara ada mgbe niile, gụnyere n’oge ọdịda; nhazi azịza ya na-esokwa R1/R2. Redis dị mma anaghị ede pins mpaghara: enweghị mirror ma ọ bụ ledger nhazi, ma instances nwere ike ịdị iche n’oge ọdịda.

Endpoint oke ojiji nke onye na-enye ọrụ, ọwara netwọk (Tailscale, Ngrok, MITM debug proxy), ọdọ mmiri egress nke 1Proxy, ndabere nchekwa data na obere mgbanwe kwa-feature ndị executor layer ma ọ bụ scripts na-ezo aka na ha.

| Mgbanwe                                        | Ndabara                                                                     | Faịlụ Isi                                                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SESSION_AFFINITY_STORE`             | `sqlite`                                                                    | `src/lib/db/sessionAffinity/storeFactory.ts`                              | Backend maka njikọ session na akaụntụ. Naanị `redis` tinyere `REDIS_URL` ahaziri nke ọma na-eme ka e nwee nkekọrịta; uru a na-edozighị ma ọ bụ nke a na-amaghị, ma ọ bụ nhazi Redis na-efu, na-eji SQLite. Ọ chọrọ `sessionAffinityTtlMs` nke dị mma karịa efu. Nhazi Redis na-echekwa njirimara akaụntụ eji eme ihe nke a kpụchara hash tinyere NJ njikọ; nhazi SQLite ndabara na-edobe ọdịdị JSON ochie nwere mpaghara anọ. Ọ na-adakọ njirimara n'ime ọdọ mmiri mpaghara tozuru etozu tupu NJ. Ọ na-eji `REDIS_KEY_PREFIX` nke onye ahịa nkekọrịta, ụkpụrụ JSON na `PX` TTL; mgbanwe na-atụnyere uru a hụrụ kpọmkwem. Azịza Redis dị mma na-ewepụ fallback mpaghara nke nkwụsị ọrụ, azịza bịara n'oge na-adịghị anya nwekwara ike ihichapụ naanị ahịrị fallback serialized nke kpọmkwem ọrụ ahụ gafere oge dere. A na-enyocha pins e bubatara tupu eji ha ọzọ. Njehie Redis, oge gafere ma ọ bụ saturation nnabata na-alaghachi na SQLite site na ịdọ aka ná ntị ndị a na-amachi ugboro ha (oke 100 ms kwa oku store); Redis misses gara nke ọma anaghị agụ shadow. Ọ naghị ekekọrịta combo stickiness, native Codex turn pins, concurrency akaụntụ, lockouts ma ọ bụ oke mmefu. |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Eriri njikọ Redis maka backend rate limiter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(onye nnabata mmepụta)_                                                    | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Gbanwee onye nnabata a na-eji nweta oke coding-plan Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

| `MITM_CERT_MODE`                               | `legacy`                                                                    | `src/mitm/manager.ts`, `src/mitm/server.cjs`                              | Onye njikwa MITM na-edobe ya maka usoro proxy a malitere (`root-ca` \| `legacy`) — ọ na-egosipụta mkpebi mgbanwe cert; ebughị ya n'obi ka aka tinye ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_SUDO`                            | `0`                                                                         | `src/mitm/systemCommands.ts`                                              | Tọọ `1` (truthy) iji wepụ `sudo` nke dị n’ihu na iwu ntụkwasị obi asambodo MITM — maka deployments na-enweghị root / ndị na-eji user namespace ebe onye ọrụ na-atụkwasị CA ahụ obi n’onwe ya (dịka site na usoro Node nke extra-CA-certs).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DNS_WRITE`                     | _(edobebeghị)_                                                              | `src/mitm/dns/dnsConfig.ts`                                               | Tọọ `1` ka ịwụpụ ide na faịlụ hosts mgbe a na-agbakwunye ma ọ bụ na-ewepụ ndekọ DNS — maka gburugburu ule sandbox ma ọ bụ nke a na-agụghị ede.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

A na-ewepụ slash dị n’ọgwụgwụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

Ọ na-eji `8080` dị ka ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SINGBOX_PORT`                                 | `20140`                                                                     | `src/lib/services/bootstrap.ts`                                           | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ọrụ sing-box embedded a na-elekọta na-ejikọta na ya (`127.0.0.1:<port>`) mgbe OmniRoute na-achịkwa okirikiri ndụ sidecar sing-box. Ọ na-eji `20140` dị ka ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `LLMLINGUA_PORT`                               | `20135`                                                                     | `src/lib/services/bootstrap.ts`                                           | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ọrụ mkpakọba LLMLingua a na-elekọta na-ejikọta na ya (`127.0.0.1:<port>`). Ọ na-eji `20135` dị ka ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `LLMLINGUA_BASE_URL`                           | `http://127.0.0.1:20135`                                                    | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/llmlingua/index.ts`                | URL ntọala maka daemon microservice mkpakọba HTTP LLMLingua dị na mpaghara. Ọ na-eji `http://127.0.0.1:20135` dị ka ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `LLMLINGUA_MODEL`                              | `atjsh/llmlingua-2-js-tinybert-meetingbank`                                 | `src/lib/services/installers/llmlingua.ts`                                | NJ model Hugging Face nke sidecar LLMLingua a na-elekọta na-ebunye (a na-enyefe ya na env nke usoro nwa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `BIFROST_API_KEY`                              | _(unset)_                                                                   | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrost/route.ts`                  | Igodo API maka gateway Bifrost (a na-eziga ya dị ka `Authorization: Bearer ...`). Ọ bụrụ na edoghị ya, route ahụ na-atụ anya ka arịrịọ ahụ nwee igodo API OmniRoute ziri ezi; igodo a bụ naanị maka nyocha ikike n'akụkụ gateway.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `BIFROST_STREAMING_ENABLED`                    | `true`                                                                      | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrost/route.ts`                  | Mgbe ọ bụ true, route sidecar Bifrost na-enyefe nzaghachi azụ site na SSE site na gateway kama iji TS streaming executor. Tọọ ya na `0` iji manye nzaghachi JSON na-enweghị streaming site na gateway.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `BIFROST_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | `30000`                                                                     | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrost/route.ts`                  | Oge nkwụsị kwa-arịrịọ mgbe a na-ebufe arịrịọ gaa gateway Bifrost (ms). Mgbe oge nkwụsị gafere, route ahụ na-eweghachite ụzọ relay TS site na isiokwu `X-Bifrost-Fallback`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_BIFROST_KEY`                        | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrost/route.ts`                  | Aha ọzọ maka `BIFROST_API_KEY` (scripts na-agụ env site na `OMNIROUTE_*` na-eji ya). `BIFROST_API_KEY` na-ebute ụzọ mgbe edobere ha abụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND`                      | `ts` / `auto`                                                               | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/routingBackend.ts`                 | Relay backend maka `/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`: `ts \| bifrost \| auto`. `ts` = relay TypeScript (ndabara mgbe ahazighị Bifrost); `auto` na-ahọrọ Bifrost mgbe edobere `BIFROST_BASE_URL` ma `BIFROST_ENABLED` ≠ `0`, yana ọ na-alaghachi na TS na akpaghị aka ma sidecar enweghị ike iru; `bifrost` na-amanye Bifrost (siri ike, enweghị fallback). Auth/rate-limit/injection-guard/allowlist na-arụ ọrụ mgbe niile na Next route mbụ. Nzaghachi na-ebu `X-Routing-Backend` / `X-Routing-Fallback` / `X-Routing-Fallback-Reason`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `RELAY_ROUTING_BACKEND`                        | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/routingBackend.ts`                 | Aha ọzọ anabatara maka `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND` (otu ụkpụrụ `ts \| bifrost \| auto` ahụ). `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND` na-ebute ụzọ mgbe edobere ha abụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_BIFROST_FAILURE_COOLDOWN_MS`        | `5000`                                                                      | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrostCooldown.ts`                | Oge nkwụsị (ms) mgbe hop Bifrost sidecar dara na ọnọdụ `auto`, tupu relay anwaghachi ịnwale sidecar; ọ na-ebugharị ozugbo gaa n'ụzọ TS mgbe oge nkwụsị ahụ ka na-aga, mesịa nyochaa ọzọ. `0` na-ewepụ ya. Ọ na-emetụta naanị mgbe `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND=auto`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`                           | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Ụzọ gaa na asambodo PEM TLS iji jiri HTTPS jee ozi `omniroute serve` (dakọtara na `--tls-cert`). A ghaghị ijikọta ya na `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`; server standalone ahụ na-akwụsị TLS n'otu listener ahụ (`wss://` na-arụkwa ọrụ n'enweghị mgbanwe). Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya → HTTP nkịtị. Inye naanị otu n'ime cert/key, ma ọ bụ inye ụzọ a na-apụghị ịgụ, na-edekọ ịdọ aka ná ntị ma na-anọgide na HTTP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`                            | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Ụzọ gaa na igodo nzuzo PEM TLS maka HTTPS nke `omniroute serve` (dakọtara na `--tls-key`). A ghaghị ijikọta ya na `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`. Lee `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED`            | `0`                                                                         | `src/lib/security/localEndpoints.ts`                                      | Mgba ọkụ bụ isi maka ụzọ `/api/local/*`. Mgbe edobeghị ya ma ọ bụ ọ bụ `0`, ụzọ `/api/local/*` niile na-eweghachi 503 na production. Ọ ghaghị ịbụ `1` na deployments na-abụghị loopback iji mee ka Redis launcher na ndị na-amalite ọrụ mpaghara yiri ya nke otu-pịa rụọ ọrụ. Ọ na-arụkọ ọrụ dị ka nchedo abụọ na nhazi route-guard nke `isLocalOnlyPath()` (`LOCAL_ONLY_API_PREFIXES` dị na `src/server/authz/routeGuard.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_TOKEN`              | _(edobeghị)_                                                                | `src/lib/security/localEndpoints.ts`                                      | Bearer token maka ndị na-akpọ `/api/local/*` na-anọghị na loopback (dịka desktop app). Mgbe edobere ya, arịrịọ sitere na IP ndị na-abụghị loopback ga-enwerịrị `Authorization: Bearer <token>`. A chọrọ ya mgbe `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED=1` na deployments na-abụghị loopback.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_CONTAINER_NAME`               | `omniroute-redis`                                                           | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Aha akpa maka onye na-ebido Redis otu-pịa (`omniroute redis up`). Ma CLI ma GUI `RedisLauncherPanel` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_HOST_PORT`                    | `6379`                                                                      | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Port onye nnabata maka onye na-ebido Redis otu-pịa. Gbanwee ya ma ọ bụrụ na onye nnabata ejirila 6379 jikọọ. Port dị n'ime akpa ahụ ka bụ 6379.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST`                    | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Interface onye nnabata nke onye na-ebido Redis otu-pịa na-ebipụta na ya. Onye na-ebido ya na-amalite Redis NA-ENWEGHỊ paswọọdụ, yabụ ijikọ na `0.0.0.0` na-enye kọmputa nnabata ọ bụla dị na LAN gị Redis na-enweghị nkwenye — gbasaa ya naanị ma ọ bụrụ na gị onwe gị tinyekwara paswọọdụ na instance ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Interface onye nnabata nke docker-compose na-ebipụta Redis sidecar na ya (#9286). Redis compose na-agba ọsọ na-enweghị `requirepass`; akpa ngwa na-erute ya site na netwọkụ compose (`redis:6379`) — port a na-ebipụta dị naanị maka ngwaọrụ ndị dị n’akụkụ onye nnabata. `0.0.0.0` na-ekpughere LAN niile Redis na-enweghị nkwenye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `REDIS_PORT`                                   | `6379`                                                                      | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Port onye nnabata maka Redis sidecar nke compose.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `APP_BIND_HOST`                                | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`, `docker-compose.prod.yml`                           | Interface onye nnabata nke docker-compose na-ebipụta dashboard/API/live-WS nke ngwa ahụ na ya (#12568). Ebe `REQUIRE_API_KEY=false` bụ ndabara `.env.example` a na-ebufe, `0.0.0.0` na-ekpughere proxy LLM `/v1` na-enweghị aha nye LAN/WAN niile — gbasaa ya naanị mgbe `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` dị ma ọ bụ mgbe reverse proxy dị n’ihu na-amanye nkwenye nke ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `QDRANT_BIND_HOST`                             | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Interface onye nnabata nke docker-compose na-ebipụta Qdrant memory sidecar na ya (#12578). Otu ihe kpatara nchegbu banyere ikpughe na LAN dịkwa maka `REDIS_BIND_HOST`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `BIFROST_BIND_HOST`                            | `127.0.0.1`                                                                 | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Interface onye nnabata nke docker-compose na-ebipụta Bifrost router sidecar na ya (#12578). | `REDIS_KEY_PREFIX`                             | `omniroute:`                                                                | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Prefix oghere aha a na-etinye n’isi igodo Redis OmniRoute ọ bụla (rate limiter, auth cache, quota store, warmup circuit breaker). Ọ na-egbochi esemokwu igodo mgbe a na-ekekọrịta Redis instance ahụ na ngwa ndị ọzọ (#11042).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`             | _(unset — mechanism disabled)_                                              | `src/lib/api/internalServiceAuth.ts`                                      | Nzuzo e kesara maka hops REST nke ime na-echekwa njirimara (#9260): akụkụ OmniRoute na-akpọ ụzọ OmniRoute mpaghara ndị ọzọ na-eziga ya dị ka `x-omniroute-internal-service-token` ka e wee chekwaa njirimara onye kpọrọ oku mbụ. A na-atụnyere ya site na `timingSafeEqual`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE`        | _(unset)_                                                                   | `src/lib/api/internalServiceAuth.ts`                                      | Ụdị faịlụ nzuzo nke akara ọrụ ime: ụzọ gaa na faịlụ nke ọdịnaya e wepụrụ oghere n’akụkụ ya bụ akara ahụ. A na-enyocha ya naanị mgbe inline var adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED`            | `true`                                                                      | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterProviderStats.ts`                              | Tinye stats nhazi kwa izu nke OpenRouter na ndepụta ndị na-enye ọrụ nke dashboard (#9324). Ọ na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara; tọọ `false` ka ịwụpụ fetch nke ndabere kpamkpam (ọ naghị egbochi ọrụ, ọ dịghịkwa ada ada).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`             | `86400000` (24h)                                                            | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterProviderStats.ts`                              | TTL cache maka snapshot stats ndị na-enye ọrụ OpenRouter, n’ime milliseconds.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_IMAGE`                        | `redis:7-alpine`                                                            | `bin/cli/commands/redis.mjs`                                              | Redis image nke 1-click Redis launcher na-eji. Gbanwee ya gaa na `redis:8-alpine` ma ọ bụ mirror registry nkeonwe dịka ọ dị mkpa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `QDRANT_HOST`                                  | `qdrant`                                                                    | _(opt-in cluster profile)_                                                | Aha host nke Qdrant sidecar mgbe `--profile memory` na-arụ ọrụ. A na-eji ya naanị mgbe `qdrantEnabled` bụ `true` n’ime koodu (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts:108`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QDRANT_PORT`                                  | `6333`                                                                      | _(opt-in cluster profile)_                                                | REST port nke Qdrant sidecar.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |

Ọ bụ ọbaakwụkwọ ndị ahịa na-eji mgbe ha họọrọ gRPC karịa REST maka ọrụ nkwanye data.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QDRANT_API_KEY`                               | _(unset)_                                                                   | _(profa ụyọkọ opt-in)_                                                    | Igodo API nhọrọ maka Qdrant Cloud ma ọ bụ ihe atụ on-prem nke chọrọ nyocha. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ → a naghị eziga isi `api-key`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QDRANT_COLLECTION`                            | `omniroute-memory`                                                          | _(profa ụyọkọ opt-in)_                                                    | Aha mkpokọta maka embeddings ebe nchekwa mkparịta ụka OmniRoute.
